/****** 物件:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[GetReportDate]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date, ,>
-- Description:	<Description, ,>
-- =============================================
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[GetReportDate]
(
	-- Add the parameters for the function here
	@Date datetime,
	@Timezone decimal(18, 4) = 8
)
RETURNS datetime
AS
BEGIN
	-- Declare the return variable here
	DECLARE @Year int
	DECLARE @Month int
	DECLARE @Day int
	DECLARE @Hour int
	DECLARE @RetValue datetime
	DECLARE @UTCDate datetime
	DECLARE @TargetDate datetime

	SET @UTCDate = DATEADD(HOUR, -8, @Date)
	SET @TargetDate = DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, @UTCDate)

	SELECT @Year = YEAR(@TargetDate), @Month=MONTH(@TargetDate), @Day=DAY(@TargetDate)

	SET @RetValue = DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day)

	-- Return the result of the function
	RETURN @RetValue
END
GO
/****** 物件:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[GetReportDateAndTime]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[GetReportDateAndTime]
(
    -- Add the parameters for the function here
    @Date datetime,
    @Timezone decimal(18, 4) = 8
)
RETURNS datetime
AS
BEGIN
    -- Declare the return variable here
    DECLARE @Year int
    DECLARE @Month int
    DECLARE @Day int
    DECLARE @Hour int
    DECLARE @Minute int
    DECLARE @Second int
    DECLARE @Millisecond int
    DECLARE @RetValue datetime

    DECLARE @UTCDate datetime
    DECLARE @TargetDate datetime

    SET @UTCDate = DATEADD(HOUR, -8, @Date)
    SET @TargetDate = DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, @UTCDate)

    SELECT @Year = YEAR(@TargetDate), @Month = MONTH(@TargetDate), @Day = DAY(@TargetDate),
           @Hour = DATEPART(HOUR, @TargetDate), @Minute = DATEPART(MINUTE, @TargetDate),
           @Second = DATEPART(SECOND, @TargetDate), @Millisecond = DATEPART(MILLISECOND, @TargetDate)

    SET @RetValue = DATETIMEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day, @Hour, @Minute, @Second, @Millisecond)

    -- Return the result of the function
    RETURN @RetValue
END
GO
/****** 物件:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[GetReportMonth]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date, ,>
-- Description:	<Description, ,>
-- =============================================
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[GetReportMonth]
(
	-- Add the parameters for the function here
	@Date datetime
)
RETURNS int
AS
BEGIN
	-- Declare the return variable here
	DECLARE @Year int
	DECLARE @Month int
	DECLARE @Day int
	DECLARE @Hour int
	DECLARE @ReportDate datetime

	SELECT @Year = YEAR(@Date), @Month=MONTH(@Date), @Day=DAY(@Date), @Hour = DATEPART(hh, @Date)

	IF (@Hour>=12)
	BEGIN
		SET @ReportDate = DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day)
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ReportDate = DATEADD(d, -1, DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day))
	END

	-- Return the result of the function
	RETURN MONTH(@ReportDate)

END


GO
/****** 物件:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[GetReportYear]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO





-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date, ,>
-- Description:	<Description, ,>
-- =============================================
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[GetReportYear]
(
	-- Add the parameters for the function here
	@Date datetime
)
RETURNS int
AS
BEGIN
	-- Declare the return variable here
	DECLARE @Year int
	DECLARE @Month int
	DECLARE @Day int
	DECLARE @Hour int
	DECLARE @ReportDate datetime

	SELECT @Year = YEAR(@Date), @Month=MONTH(@Date), @Day=DAY(@Date), @Hour = DATEPART(hh, @Date)

	IF (@Hour>=12)
	BEGIN
		SET @ReportDate = DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day)
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ReportDate = DATEADD(d, -1, DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, @Day))
	END

	-- Return the result of the function
	RETURN YEAR(@ReportDate)

END



GO
/****** 物件:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[IsServiceDate]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date, ,>
-- Description:	<Description, ,>
-- =============================================
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[IsServiceDate]
(
	-- Add the parameters for the function here
	@Timezone decimal(18, 4),
	@ServiceStartDate datetime,  -- 只接受時間
	@ServiceEndDate datetime
)
RETURNS bit
AS
BEGIN
	-- Declare the return variable here
	DECLARE @CurrentDate datetime
	DECLARE @StartDate datetime
	DECLARE @EndDate datetime
	DECLARE @InServiceDate bit

	SET @InServiceDate=0
	--SET @Timezone=8
	--SET @ServiceStartDate='00:00:00'
	--SET @ServiceEndDate='13:44:59'

	SET @CurrentDate = CAST(DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, GETUTCDATE()) AS TIME)
	SET @StartDate = CAST(@ServiceStartDate AS TIME)  -- 避免帶入日期
	SET @EndDate = CAST(@ServiceEndDate AS TIME)

	IF (@StartDate < @EndDate)
	BEGIN
		-- 順向判斷, 例如 00:00:00 - 07:59:59
		IF (@CurrentDate>=@StartDate AND @CurrentDate<(DATEADD(SECOND, 1, @EndDate)))
			SET @InServiceDate=1
	END
	ELSE IF (@StartDate > @EndDate)
	BEGIN
		-- 跨日, 例如 23:00:00 - 02:59:59
		IF ((@CurrentDate>=@StartDate AND @CurrentDate<(DATEADD(SECOND, 1, '23:59:59'))) OR
		    (@CurrentDate>='00:00:00' AND @CurrentDate<(DATEADD(SECOND, 1, @EndDate))))
			SET @InServiceDate=1
	END
	ELSE
		SET @InServiceDate=1  -- 開始時間與結束時間相等, 直接判斷正在服務中

	RETURN @InServiceDate
END
GO

/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddCompanyManual]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddCompanyManual]
	--0代收 --1代付
	@Type int,
	@ServiceType varchar(20),
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@Description nvarchar(200),
	@TransactionSerial varchar(50), 
	@forCompanyID int,
	@forAdminID int
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=後台操作人員不存在
	-- -2=商戶錢包不存在
	-- -3=商戶支付方式有誤
	-- -4=缺少相關說明
	-- -5=鎖定失敗
	-- -6=異動記錄新增失敗
	-- -7=加扣點失敗	
	
	--CHECK COUNT
	DECLARE @CheckCount	int
	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--ID
	DECLARE @CompanyManualID int
	DECLARE @CompanyManuaSerial varchar(200)

	DECLARE @TranCount int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int


	--CHECK ADMIN ID
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM AdminTable WHERE AdminID = @forAdminID

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -1

	--CHECK COMPANY ID AND POINT
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM CompanyPoint WHERE forCompanyID = @forCompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -2

	--CHECK COMPANY SERVICE
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM CompanyService WHERE forCompanyID = @forCompanyID AND ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -3

	--CHECK DESCRIPTION
	IF(@Description IS NULL)
		RETURN -4
		
	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT

		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN CompanyManual			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN CompanyManual
		
		SET @LockName = 'AddCompanyManual'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet >= 0)
		BEGIN
			

			INSERT INTO CompanyManualHistory (ServiceType, forCompanyID, forAdminID, Type, Description, Amount, CurrencyType, TransactionSerial)
			VALUES (@ServiceType, @forCompanyID, @forAdminID, @Type, @Description, @Amount, @CurrencyType, @TransactionSerial)

			SET @CompanyManualID = @@IDENTITY
			SET @PointDescription = 'AddCompanyManual,CompanyManual=' + CONVERT(varchar, @CompanyManualID)

			IF(@CompanyManualID IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			   SET @CompanyManuaSerial='CM'+FORMAT(GETDATE(),'yyyyMMddHHmm')+Right('00000000' + Cast(@CompanyManualID as varchar),10) 

			   UPDATE CompanyManualHistory SET TransactionSerial =@CompanyManuaSerial WHERE CompanyManualID=@CompanyManualID AND ISNULL(TransactionSerial, '') = ''
			 
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @forCompanyID, @CurrencyType, 3, @Amount, @CompanyManualID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType

				IF(@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
					IF(@TranCount = 0)
					BEGIN
						COMMIT 
					END
					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyManual
					RETURN -7
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
			    ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyManual
				RETURN -6
			END
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyManual
			RETURN -5
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddCompanyPoint]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddCompanyPoint]
	@CompanyID int,
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@OperatorType int,
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200),
	@ServiceType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	DECLARE @GUID varchar(50)

	SELECT @SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
	FROM CompanyServicePoint 
	WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND ServiceType=@ServiceType

	IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
	BEGIN
	  	INSERT INTO CompanyServicePoint(CurrencyType, CompanyID, SystemPointValue, ServiceType)
		VALUES (@CurrencyType, @CompanyID, 0, @ServiceType)
		
		SELECT @SystemPointValue=0
	END
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=商户钱包不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	-- -3=加扣点记录已存在
	-- -4=商户支付通道不存在
	DECLARE @TranCount int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	
	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT

		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN CompanyPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN CompanyPoint

		SET @LockName = 'AddCompanyPoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT @GUID = CompanyPointHistoryID
			FROM  CompanyPointHistory
			WHERE TransactionID=@TransactionID AND OperatorType=@OperatorType

			IF(@GUID IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO CompanyPointHistory(forCompanyID, CurrencyType, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, ValueByService, BeforeValueByService, Description, TransactionID, ServiceType)
				SELECT @CompanyID, ISNULL(P.CurrencyType, @CurrencyType), @OperatorType, @Amount, P.PointValue, @Amount, P.SystemPointValue, @Description, @TransactionID, @ServiceType
				FROM
				(
					SELECT CSP.CurrencyType, PointValue, CSP.SystemPointValue
					FROM CompanyPoint CP 
					LEFT JOIN CompanyServicePoint CSP ON CSP.CompanyID = CP.forCompanyID AND CSP. CurrencyType = CP.CurrencyType AND ServiceType=@ServiceType
					WHERE forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND CSP.CurrencyType=@CurrencyType
				) AS P

				UPDATE CompanyPoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET PointValue=PointValue+@Amount WHERE forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
				BEGIN			
					ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
					RETURN -1
				END

				UPDATE CompanyServicePoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue+@Amount WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType  AND ServiceType=@ServiceType
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
				BEGIN			
					ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
					RETURN -4
				END

				IF(@TranCount = 0)
				BEGIN
					COMMIT 
				END

				RETURN 0
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
				RETURN -3
			END
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddCompanyServicePoint]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddCompanyServicePoint]
	@CompanyID int,
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@OperatorType int,
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200),
	@ServiceType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4) 

	SELECT @SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
	FROM CompanyServicePoint 
	WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND ServiceType=@ServiceType

	IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
	BEGIN
	  	INSERT INTO CompanyServicePoint(CurrencyType, CompanyID, SystemPointValue, ServiceType)
		VALUES (@CurrencyType, @CompanyID, 0, @ServiceType)
		
		SELECT @SystemPointValue=0
	END
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @TranCount int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	
	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT

		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN CompanyPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN CompanyPoint

		SET @LockName = 'AddCompanyServicePoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
				INSERT INTO CompanyPointHistory(forCompanyID, CurrencyType, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, ValueByService, BeforeValueByService, Description, TransactionID, ServiceType)
				SELECT @CompanyID, ISNULL(P.CurrencyType, @CurrencyType), @OperatorType, 0, P.PointValue, @Amount, P.SystemPointValue, @Description, @TransactionID, @ServiceType
				FROM
				(
					SELECT CSP.CurrencyType, PointValue, CSP.SystemPointValue
					FROM CompanyPoint CP WITH (NOWAIT)
					LEFT JOIN CompanyServicePoint CSP WITH (NOWAIT) ON CSP.CompanyID = CP.forCompanyID AND CSP. CurrencyType = CP.CurrencyType AND ServiceType=@ServiceType
					WHERE forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND CSP.CurrencyType=@CurrencyType
				) AS P

				UPDATE CompanyServicePoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue+@Amount WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType  AND ServiceType=@ServiceType
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
				BEGIN			
					ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
					RETURN -1
				END

				IF(@TranCount = 0)
				BEGIN
					COMMIT 
				END

				RETURN 0
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN CompanyPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddManual]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddManual]
	--0代收 --1代付
	@Type int,
	@ServiceType varchar(20),
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@Description nvarchar(200),
	@TransactionSerial varchar(50), 
    @ProviderCode varchar(50), 
	@forCompanyID int,
	@forAdminID int
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=後台操作人員不存在
	-- -2=商戶錢包不存在
	-- -3=商戶支付方式有誤
	-- -4=缺少相關說明
	-- -5=鎖定失敗
	-- -6=異動記錄新增失敗
	-- -7=加扣點失敗	
	-- -8=供應商不存在	
	-- -9=供應商錢包不存在	
	-- -10=供應商加扣點失敗
	-- -11=供應商異動記錄新增失敗
	--CHECK COUNT
	DECLARE @CheckCount	int
	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--ID
	DECLARE @CompanyManualID int
	DECLARE @CompanyManuaSerial varchar(200)
	DECLARE @ProviderManualID int
	DECLARE @ProviderManuaSerial varchar(200)

	DECLARE @TranCount int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int


	--CHECK ADMIN ID
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM AdminTable WHERE AdminID = @forAdminID

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -1

	--CHECK COMPANY ID AND POINT
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM CompanyPoint WHERE forCompanyID = @forCompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -2

	--CHECK COMPANY SERVICE
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM CompanyService WHERE forCompanyID = @forCompanyID AND ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -3

	--CHECK DESCRIPTION
	IF(@Description IS NULL)
		RETURN -4

	--CHECK ProviderCode
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM ProviderCode WHERE ProviderCode = @ProviderCode
	
	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -8

	--CHECK ProviderPoint
	SELECT @CheckCount=COUNT(*)
	FROM ProviderPoint WHERE ProviderCode = @ProviderCode And CurrencyType=@CurrencyType

	IF(@CheckCount=0)
		RETURN -9
		
	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT

		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN Manual			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN Manual
		
		SET @LockName = 'AddManual'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet >= 0)
		BEGIN
			

			INSERT INTO CompanyManualHistory (ServiceType, forCompanyID, forAdminID, Type, Description, Amount, CurrencyType, TransactionSerial)
			VALUES (@ServiceType, @forCompanyID, @forAdminID, @Type, @Description, @Amount, @CurrencyType, @TransactionSerial)

			SET @CompanyManualID = @@IDENTITY
			SET @PointDescription = 'AddCompanyManual,CompanyManual=' + CONVERT(varchar, @CompanyManualID)

			IF(@CompanyManualID IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			   SET @CompanyManuaSerial='CM'+FORMAT(GETDATE(),'yyyyMMddHHmm')+Right('00000000' + Cast(@CompanyManualID as varchar),10) 

			   UPDATE CompanyManualHistory SET TransactionSerial =@CompanyManuaSerial WHERE CompanyManualID=@CompanyManualID
			 
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @forCompanyID, @CurrencyType, 3, @Amount, @CompanyManualID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType

				IF(@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
				    	INSERT INTO ProviderManualHistory (ProviderCode, forAdminID, Type, Description, Amount, CurrencyType, TransactionSerial)
						VALUES (@ProviderCode, @forAdminID, @Type, @Description, @Amount, @CurrencyType, @TransactionSerial)

						SET @ProviderManualID = @@IDENTITY
						SET @PointDescription = 'AddProviderManual,ProviderManual=' + CONVERT(varchar, @ProviderManualID)

						IF(@ProviderManualID IS NOT NULL)
						BEGIN
						   SET @ProviderManuaSerial='PM'+FORMAT(GETDATE(),'yyyyMMddHHmm')+Right('00000000' + Cast(@ProviderManualID as varchar),10) 

						   UPDATE ProviderManualHistory SET TransactionSerial =@ProviderManuaSerial WHERE ProviderManualID=@ProviderManualID
			    
						   EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPointByManual @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 3, @Amount, @ProviderManualID, @PointDescription

							IF(@PointRet = 0)
							BEGIN

								IF(@TranCount = 0)
								BEGIN
									COMMIT 
								END

								RETURN 0
							END
							ELSE
							BEGIN
								ROLLBACK TRAN Manual
								RETURN -10
							END
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							ROLLBACK TRAN Manual
							RETURN -11
						END
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN Manual
					RETURN -7
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
			    ROLLBACK TRAN Manual
				RETURN -6
			END
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN Manual
			RETURN -5
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddProviderManual]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddProviderPoint]
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@OperatorType int,
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4),
	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @GUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @TotalDepositePointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @AddTotalDepositePointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TotalProfitPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TranCount int

	BEGIN
	    IF @OperatorType=2
		BEGIN
		  SET @AddTotalDepositePointValue=0
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		  SET @AddTotalDepositePointValue=@Amount
		END

		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT
		
		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN ProviderPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN ProviderPoint

		SET @LockName = 'AddProviderPoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT @GUID = ProviderPointHistoryID
			FROM  ProviderPointHistory
			WHERE TransactionID=@TransactionID AND OperatorType=@OperatorType

			IF(@GUID IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				SELECT @SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
				FROM ProviderPoint 
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
				BEGIN
				  	INSERT INTO ProviderPoint(CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SystemPointValue)
					VALUES (@CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0)
					SELECT @SystemPointValue=0,@TotalProfitPointValue=0,@TotalDepositePointValue=0
				END
	      
				INSERT INTO ProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, Description, TransactionID)
				SELECT                          @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @OperatorType, @Amount, @SystemPointValue, @Description, @TransactionID
		
				UPDATE ProviderPoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET TotalDepositePointValue=TotalDepositePointValue+@AddTotalDepositePointValue,
														SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue+@Amount,
														TotalProfitPointValue=TotalProfitPointValue+@ProfitAmount
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					IF(@TranCount = 0)
					BEGIN
						COMMIT 
					END

					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
					RETURN -1
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
				RETURN -3
			END			
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO

	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200)
AS

	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TotalProfitPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TranCount int

	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT
		
		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN ProviderPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN ProviderPoint
	
		SET @LockName = 'AddProviderPoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

				SELECT @SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
				FROM ProviderPoint 
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
				BEGIN
				  	INSERT INTO ProviderPoint(CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SystemPointValue)
					VALUES (@CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0)
					SELECT @SystemPointValue=0,@TotalProfitPointValue=0,@TotalDepositePointValue=0
				END
	      
				INSERT INTO ProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, Description, TransactionID)
				SELECT                          @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @OperatorType, @Amount, @SystemPointValue, @Description, @TransactionID
		
				UPDATE ProviderPoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue+@Amount
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					IF(@TranCount = 0)
					BEGIN
						COMMIT 
					END

					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
					RETURN -1
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
				RETURN -3
			END			
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END

-- =============================================

	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@OperatorType int,
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200),
	@ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @GUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @TotalDepositePointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TotalProfitPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TranCount int

	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT
		
		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN ProviderPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN ProviderPoint
	
		SET @LockName = 'AddProviderPoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT @GUID = ProviderPointHistoryID
			FROM  ProviderPointHistory
			WHERE TransactionID=@TransactionID AND OperatorType=@OperatorType

			IF(@GUID IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				SELECT @SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
				FROM ProviderPoint 
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
				BEGIN
				  	INSERT INTO ProviderPoint(CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SystemPointValue)
					VALUES (@CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0)
					SELECT @SystemPointValue=0,@TotalProfitPointValue=0,@TotalDepositePointValue=0
				END
	      
				INSERT INTO ProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, Description, TransactionID)
				SELECT                          @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @OperatorType, @Amount, @SystemPointValue, @Description, @TransactionID
		
				UPDATE ProviderPoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue+@Amount,TotalProfitPointValue=TotalProfitPointValue+@ProfitAmount,TotalDepositePointValue=TotalDepositePointValue+@Amount
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					IF(@TranCount = 0)
					BEGIN
						COMMIT 
					END

					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
					RETURN -1
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
				RETURN -3
			END			
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddProviderPrePaidPoint]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddProviderPrePaidPoint]
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
    @OperatorType int,
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@TransactionID int, 
	@Description nvarchar(200)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @GUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @TranCount int
	DECLARE @PrePaidPoint decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @SystemPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	BEGIN
		SET @TranCount=@@TRANCOUNT
		
		IF(@TranCount = 0)
			BEGIN TRAN ProviderPrePaidPoint			
		ELSE
			SAVE TRAN ProviderPrePaidPoint

		SET @LockName = 'AddProviderPrePaidPoint'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
	
			IF(1=1)
			BEGIN
			    SELECT @PrePaidPoint=PrePaidPoint,@SystemPointValue=SystemPointValue
				FROM ProviderPoint 
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				IF @SystemPointValue IS NULL
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO ProviderPoint(CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SystemPointValue,PrePaidPoint)
					VALUES (@CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0,0)
					SELECT @PrePaidPoint=0
				END

				UPDATE ProviderPoint WITH (ROWLOCK) SET PrePaidPoint=PrePaidPoint+@Amount
				WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CurrencyType=@CurrencyType 

				INSERT INTO ProviderPrePaidHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue, Description, TransactionID)
				SELECT                            @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @OperatorType, @Amount, @PrePaidPoint, @Description, @TransactionID
	      
			

				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					IF(@TranCount = 0)
					BEGIN
						COMMIT 
					END

					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPrePaidPoint
					RETURN -1
				END		
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPrePaidPoint
				RETURN -3
			END		

        
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK TRAN ProviderPrePaidPoint
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spAddWithdrawalByBackendDownData]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spAddWithdrawalByBackendDownData] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@WithdrawType int,
	@forCompanyID int,
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@ServiceType varchar(20),
	@Amount decimal(18, 4),
	@CollectCharge decimal(18, 4),
    @CollectRate decimal(18, 4),
	@CostCharge decimal(18, 4),
	@Status int,
	@BankCard varchar(50),
	@BankCardName nvarchar(20),
	@BankName nvarchar(50),
	@BankBranchName nvarchar(100),
	@OwnProvince nvarchar(100),
	@OwnCity nvarchar(100),
	@DownStatus int,
	@FloatType int,
	@IsBlockChainOrder int,
	@OKEXRate decimal(18, 4),
	@DownBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4),
	@WithdrawID int OUT


AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=鎖定失敗
	-- -4=餘額不足

	
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @CompanyDescription nvarchar(MAX)
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	
	DECLARE @CompanyCheck int
	
	--BEGIN
	--IF(@DownOrderID IS NOT NULL)
	--BEGIN
	BEGIN TRAN

	SET @LockName = 'AddWithdrawalByDownData'
	EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 10000
	IF (@LockRet>=0)
	BEGIN	

	SET @CompanyCheck = 0

	--查询商户备注
	SELECT @CompanyDescription = Description,@Timezone=Timezone
	FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK) 
	WHERE CompanyID=@forCompanyID 
			
	DECLARE @CompanyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CompanyFrozenPoint decimal(18, 4)
	--餘額
	SELECT @CompanyCanUsePoint = (CP.PointValue 
        -(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(W.Amount + W.CollectCharge+ (W.Amount*W.DownRate*0.01)),0) 
           	FROM Withdrawal W 
        	WHERE W.Status <> 2 AND W.Status <> 3 AND W.Status <> 8 AND W.Status <> 90 AND W.Status <> 91 AND W.forCompanyID = CP.forCompanyID AND W.CurrencyType = CP.CurrencyType)
        -(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(SC.SummaryNetAmount),0) 
        	FROM CompanyService CS 
        	INNER JOIN SummaryCompanyByDate SC ON CS.forCompanyID = SC.forCompanyID AND CS.CurrencyType = SC.CurrencyType AND SC.ServiceType = CS.ServiceType 
        		AND   ((CS.CheckoutType = 0 AND 1 = 0 ) 
        			    OR (CS.CheckoutType = 1 AND SC.SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(GETDATE(),@Timezone))
        			    OR (CS.CheckoutType = 2 AND DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()-1) = 7 AND 
        	                (SC.SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(GETDATE(),@Timezone) OR SC.SummaryDate =  dbo.GetReportDate(DATEADD(day, -1, getdate()),@Timezone)))
        				OR (CS.CheckoutType = 2 AND DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()-1) = 6 AND  SC.SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(GETDATE(),@Timezone)))
        	WHERE CS.forCompanyID = CP.forCompanyID AND CS.CurrencyType = CP.CurrencyType)) , 
         	@CompanyFrozenPoint = ISNULL((Select SUM(CompanyFrozenAmount) FROM FrozenPoint where FrozenPoint.forCompanyID=CP.forCompanyID AND FrozenPoint.CurrencyType=CP.CurrencyType AND FrozenPoint.Status=0),0)
        FROM CompanyPoint AS CP 
        WHERE CP.forCompanyID = @forCompanyID
        AND CP.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

		IF(@CompanyCanUsePoint IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyFrozenPoint IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
		IF(((@Amount+@CollectCharge+(@Amount*@CollectRate*0.01)) <= (@CompanyCanUsePoint - @CompanyFrozenPoint)))
		SET @CompanyCheck = 1
		END

		--支付通道餘額
		IF(@CompanyCheck = 1 AND @WithdrawType=1)
		BEGIN
		DECLARE @CompanySetviceCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
		DECLARE @CompanySetviceFrozenPoint decimal(18, 4) 
		    
		SELECT @CompanySetviceCanUsePoint = (CSP.SystemPointValue - (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(W.Amount + W.CollectCharge+(W.Amount+W.DownRate*0.01)), 0)
		FROM Withdrawal W 
		WHERE W.Status <> 2 
		AND W.Status <> 3 
		AND W.Status <> 90 
		AND W.Status <> 91 
		AND W.forCompanyID = CSP.CompanyID 
		AND W.ServiceType = CSP.ServiceType 
		AND W.CurrencyType = CSP.CurrencyType)) , 
		@CompanySetviceFrozenPoint = ISNULL((Select SUM(CompanyFrozenAmount) FROM 
		FrozenPoint where FrozenPoint.forCompanyID = CSP.CompanyID 
		AND FrozenPoint.CurrencyType = CSP.CurrencyType 
		AND FrozenPoint.ServiceType = CSP.ServiceType 
		AND FrozenPoint.Status = 0),0)
		FROM CompanyServicePoint AS CSP
		LEFT JOIN ServiceType ON CSP.ServiceType=ServiceType.ServiceType
		LEFT JOIN WithdrawLimit ON WithdrawLimit.ServiceType=CSP.ServiceType And WithdrawLimit.CurrencyType= CSP.CurrencyType And WithdrawLimit.WithdrawLimitType=1 And CSP.CompanyID=WithdrawLimit.forCompanyID
		WHERE CSP.CompanyID = @forCompanyID 
		AND CSP.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType 
		AND CSP.ServiceType = @ServiceType 

		IF(@CompanyCanUsePoint IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyFrozenPoint IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			IF(((@Amount+@CollectCharge+(@Amount*@CollectRate*0.01)) > (@CompanySetviceCanUsePoint - @CompanySetviceFrozenPoint)))
            SET @CompanyCheck = 0
		END
		ELSE
		SET @CompanyCheck = 0
		END

	IF(@CompanyCheck = 1)
	BEGIN
		    
		INSERT INTO Withdrawal (WithdrawType, forCompanyID, ProviderCode, CurrencyType, Amount,
        CollectCharge, CostCharge, Status, BankCard, BankCardName,
        BankName, BankBranchName, OwnProvince, OwnCity,DownRate,UpRate,
        DownStatus, FloatType,ServiceType,CompanyDescription,IsBlockChainOrder,OKEXRate,DownBlockChainRate)
        VALUES (@WithdrawType, @forCompanyID, @ProviderCode, @CurrencyType, @Amount, 
        @CollectCharge, @CostCharge, @Status, @BankCard, @BankCardName, 
        @BankName, @BankBranchName, @OwnProvince, @OwnCity,@CollectRate,0,
        @DownStatus,@FloatType,@ServiceType,@CompanyDescription,@IsBlockChainOrder,@OKEXRate,@DownBlockChainRate )
            
		SET @WithdrawID = @@IDENTITY
			
		COMMIT
		RETURN 0
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK
		RETURN -4
	END

		
	END
	ELSE
	RETURN -1
 --   END			
	--ELSE
	--	RETURN -2
 --   END
END
GO

  --      				  OR (CS.CheckoutType = 2 AND DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()-1) = 6 AND  SC.SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(GETDATE())))
  --      	   WHERE CS.forCompanyID = CP.forCompanyID AND CS.CurrencyType = CP.CurrencyType)) , 
  --       	 @CompanyFrozenPoint = ISNULL((Select SUM(CompanyFrozenAmount) FROM FrozenPoint where FrozenPoint.forCompanyID=CP.forCompanyID AND FrozenPoint.CurrencyType=CP.CurrencyType AND FrozenPoint.Status=0),0)
  --       FROM CompanyPoint AS CP

  --       AND CP.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
		 IF(1=1)
		 --IF(@CompanyCanUsePoint IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyFrozenPoint IS NOT NULL)

		 IF(1=1)

/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReview]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReview] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255)
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=锁定失败
	  -- -4=此订单非专属供应商订单
	  DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  DECLARE @FinishDate DATETIME 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
	  DECLARE @CollectType int
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(255) 



      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 

	  
      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT  @WithdrawStatus = Status,
	  	      @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			  @FinishDate=FinishDate,
			  @ProviderCode=ProviderCode
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  --取得供应商资料
      SELECT @CollectType = CollectType
      FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

	  IF(@CollectType<>1)
	  BEGIN
	      ROLLBACK	
		  RETURN -4
	  END

	  IF(@WithdrawID IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN     
	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 1 AND @FinishDate IS NULL) 
	 		BEGIN 
			 
					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 0, HandleByAdminID = 0
			        WHERE  WithdrawID = @WithdrawID AND Withdrawal.Status=1 AND FinishDate IS NULL
					
					COMMIT 
				    RETURN 0 
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReviewToFail]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReviewToFail] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255),
  @forAdminID int
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=锁定失败

	  DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  DECLARE @FinishDate DATETIME 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @CompanyID INT 

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 

	  
      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT  @WithdrawStatus = Status,
	  	      @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			  @FinishDate=FinishDate,
			  @CompanyID=forCompanyID
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  
	  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
	  FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID		


	  IF(@WithdrawID IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN     
	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 1 AND @FinishDate IS NULL) 
	 		BEGIN 
			 
					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 3, ConfirmByAdminID = @forAdminID ,FinishDate=GETDATE(),FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(GETDATE(),@Timezone)
			        WHERE  WithdrawID = @WithdrawID AND Withdrawal.Status=1 AND FinishDate IS NULL
					
					COMMIT 
				    RETURN 0 
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReviewToSuccess]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spCancelWithdrawalReviewToSuccess] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255)
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=營運商錢包有誤 
      -- -5=營運商餘額不足
      -- -6=加扣點失敗 
	  -- -7=該單非處在可充值之狀態

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 
      DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(255) 
	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 
	  --營運商額度
      DECLARE @CompanyPointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(上游)
	  DECLARE @FinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @FinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(下游)
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  DECLARE @APIDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	    --毛利
	  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  --提現單類型
	  DECLARE @WithdrawType INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
      --0=後台申請提現單=>後台審核/1=API申請代付=>後台審核/2=API申請代付=>不經後台審核
	  DECLARE @FloatType INT 
	  --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime

	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

      BEGIN TRAN 



      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
	      --取得提現單資料 
		  SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
				 @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
				 @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
				 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
				 @WithdrawType = WithdrawType,
				 @APIDidAmount = UpDidAmount,
				 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
				 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
				 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
				 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
				 @FloatType=FloatType,
				 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
				 @CreateDate=CreateDate
		  FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
		  WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

		 --查询商户备注
		  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
		  FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK) 
   		  WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID 

        --取得统计日其
		SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

		--取得公司額度 
		SELECT @CompanyPointValue = PointValue 
		FROM   CompanyPoint WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forCompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType 

			  --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawType IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 1) 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		SET @FinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CostCharge
	  	    SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CollectCharge
	  	  END	
		  ELSE IF(@WithdrawStatus = 2 OR @WithdrawStatus = 3) 	
		  BEGIN
		    COMMIT	
	  		RETURN 0	
		  END	
	  	  ELSE 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		ROLLBACK	
	  		RETURN -7	
	  	  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END

  	    IF ( @CompanyPointValue IS NOT NULL ) 
		BEGIN 
			IF ( @CompanyPointValue - @CompanyFinishtAmount >= 0 ) 
			BEGIN 
				SET @PointDescription = 'ReviewWithdrawal:WithdrawSerial=' + @WithdrawSerial 
				SET @FinishDidAmount = @FinishtAmount * -1
				SET @CompanyFinishDidAmount=@CompanyFinishtAmount * -1
				SET @ProfitAmount=@CollectCharge-@CostCharge
			    --加扣點 
			    EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 0, @CompanyFinishDidAmount, @WithdrawID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType 

			    IF ( @PointRet = 0 ) 
			    BEGIN
				    
					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 2, @FinishDidAmount,@ProfitAmount,@WithdrawID, @PointDescription
					
					IF ( @PointRet <> 0 ) 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -8
					END

					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 2, FinishDate=GETDATE(), FinishAmount = @CompanyFinishtAmount,
					FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(GETDATE(),@Timezone)
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge),
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge						
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge), @CostCharge)
					END

					
					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CollectCharge,
						SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge)			
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryWithdrawalAmount)
					                            	VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@CollectCharge,@CostCharge,@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge)
					END

			       COMMIT 
				   RETURN 0 
			    END 
				ELSE 
			    BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK 

			        UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Description = @Description + '加扣點失敗',ManagerRejectDescription='加扣點失敗'
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

			        RETURN -6
			    END 
			END 
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
				SET    Description = @Description + '餘額不足',ManagerRejectDescription='餘額不足'
				WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			    
				RETURN -5
			END 
		END 
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK 

			UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			SET    Description = @Description + '公司額度有誤',ManagerRejectDescription='公司額度有誤'
			WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			RETURN -4
		END 
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
	    ROLLBACK	
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spChangeWithdrawalGroupByAdmin]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spChangeWithdrawalGroupByAdmin] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@ChangeGroupID int,
	@WithdrawSerial varchar(50)

AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=鎖定失敗
	-- -2=訂單處理中 

	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @WithdrawalCount int
	
	BEGIN
		BEGIN TRAN

		SET @LockName = 'ChangeWithdrawalGroup'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 10000
		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN	

		--檢查訂單
   	    SELECT @WithdrawalCount=COUNT(*) 
		FROM  Withdrawal 
		JOIN  ProxyProviderOrder ON WithdrawSerial=forOrderSerial And ProxyProviderOrder.Type=1
		WHERE WithdrawSerial=@WithdrawSerial And Status=1  And  HandleByAdminID=0 

		IF(@WithdrawalCount =0)
		BEGIN
		ROLLBACK
		RETURN -2
		END
			   
			UPDATE ProxyProviderOrder
			SET ProxyProviderOrder.GroupID =@ChangeGroupID 
			FROM ProxyProviderOrder JOIN Withdrawal
			ON Withdrawal.WithdrawSerial = ProxyProviderOrder.forOrderSerial AND ProxyProviderOrder.Type = 1
			WHERE ProxyProviderOrder.forOrderSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			AND Withdrawal.Status = 1
			And Withdrawal.HandleByAdminID = 0

			IF(@@ROWCOUNT>0)
			BEGIN
				COMMIT
			RETURN 0
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				RETURN -2
			END	
	
		END
		ELSE
		RETURN -1
    END			

END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spGetAvailableBankCard_Internal]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spGetAvailableBankCard_Internal]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@CompanyID int,
	@UserIdentity nvarchar(50),
	@DirectionType int,
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
	@Amount	decimal(18, 4),
	@LockTimeoutSecond int,
	@Type int,
	@BankCardLockID varchar(50) output,
	@BankCardGUID varchar(50) output,
	@ExpireDate datetime output
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=鎖定失敗
	-- -2=無可用卡片

	DECLARE @ProviderBankCardTemp TABLE (
		BankCardGUID varchar(50),
		CardType int,
		AmountMax decimal(18, 4),
		TotalCreditValueDay decimal(18, 4),
		TotalCreditValueWeek decimal(18, 4),
		TotalCreditValueMonth decimal(18, 4),
		CreditValueDay decimal(18, 4),
		CreditValueWeek decimal(18, 4),
		CreditValueMonth decimal(18, 4),
		LastDate datetime,
		ProcessType int,
		SortKey varchar(50)
	)
	DECLARE @_BankCardGUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @_BankCardType int
	DECLARE @TotalCreditValueDay decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TotalCreditValueWeek decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @TotalCreditValueMonth decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CreditValueDay decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CreditValueWeek decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CreditValueMonth decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @FreeValueDay decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @FreeValueWeek decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @FreeValueMonth decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @LastDate datetime
	DECLARE @CurrentDate datetime
	DECLARE @RetValue int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @ExpireLockID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @ExpireLastUsedDate datetime
	DECLARE @ExpireBankCardGUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @ExpireAmount decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @ExpireString nvarchar(MAX)
	DECLARE @ExpireWhereString nvarchar(MAX)
	DECLARE @IsAvailCard int

	SELECT @Timezone = Timezone
	FROM ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK)
	WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode

	SET @CurrentDate=DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, getutcdate())

	SET @LockName = 'ProviderBankCard'
	EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Session', @LockTimeout = 5000

	IF (@LockRet>=0)
	BEGIN
		WHILE (1=1)
		BEGIN
			SET @ExpireLockID = NULL
			SET @ExpireBankCardGUID = NULL
			SET @ExpireAmount = 0
			SET @ExpireLastUsedDate = NULL
			SET @ExpireString = ''
			SET @ExpireWhereString = ''

			SELECT TOP 1 @ExpireLockID = BankCardLockID,
			             @ExpireBankCardGUID = BankCardGUID,
			             @ExpireAmount = Amount,
						 @ExpireLastUsedDate = DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, LastUsedDate)
			FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WITH (NOLOCK)
			WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND (([ExpireDate]<getutcdate()) OR ([ExpireDate] IS NULL))

			IF (@ExpireLockID IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				IF (CONVERT(varchar(20), @ExpireLastUsedDate, 23) = CONVERT(varchar(20), @CurrentDate, 23))
				BEGIN
					-- 今天額度
					IF (@ExpireWhereString <> '')
						SET @ExpireWhereString += ','

					SET @ExpireWhereString += 'CreditValueDay -= @_Amount'
				END

				IF (DATEPART(WEEK, @ExpireLastUsedDate) = DATEPART(WEEK, @CurrentDate))
				BEGIN
					-- 同一周
					IF (@ExpireWhereString <> '')
						SET @ExpireWhereString += ','

					SET @ExpireWhereString += 'CreditValueWeek -= @_Amount'
				END

				IF ((YEAR(@ExpireLastUsedDate) = YEAR(@CurrentDate)) AND (MONTH(@ExpireLastUsedDate) = MONTH(@CurrentDate)))
				BEGIN
					-- 同一月
					IF (@ExpireWhereString <> '')
						SET @ExpireWhereString += ','

					SET @ExpireWhereString += 'CreditValueMonth -= @_Amount'
				END

				IF (@ExpireWhereString <> '')
				BEGIN
					SET @ExpireString = 'UPDATE ProviderBankCard WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ' + @ExpireWhereString + ' WHERE BankCardGUID=@_BankCardGUID'

					EXEC sp_executesql @ExpireString, N'@_Amount decimal(18, 4),@_BankCardGUID varchar(50)', @_Amount = @ExpireAmount, @_BankCardGUID = @ExpireBankCardGUID
				END

				DELETE FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WHERE BankCardLockID=@ExpireLockID
			END
			ELSE
				BREAK
		END
		--DELETE FROM MemOpt_CompanyBankCardLock WHERE forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND [ExpireDate]<getutcdate()

		-- 新增專卡專用(專卡專用會跨 ProviderCode)
		INSERT INTO @ProviderBankCardTemp (BankCardGUID, CardType, AmountMax, TotalCreditValueDay, TotalCreditValueWeek, TotalCreditValueMonth, CreditValueDay, CreditValueWeek, CreditValueMonth, LastDate, ProcessType, SortKey)
		SELECT CBC.BankCardGUID, CBC.CardType, CBC.AmountMax, CBC.TotalCreditValueDay, CBC.TotalCreditValueWeek, CBC.TotalCreditValueMonth, CBC.CreditValueDay, CBC.CreditValueWeek, CBC.CreditValueMonth, CBC.LastDate, 0, '0_' + CAST(newid() AS varchar(50))
		FROM ProviderBankCard AS CBC WITH (NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN CompanyWhiteList AS CW WITH (NOLOCK) ON CW.forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND CW.UserIdentity=@UserIdentity
		INNER JOIN CompanyWhiteListProviderBankCard AS CWP WITH (NOLOCK) ON CWP.BankCardGUID=CBC.BankCardGUID AND CWP.WhiteListID=CW.WhiteListID
		WHERE CBC.BankCardState=0 AND CBC.CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND CBC.Type=@Type AND (CBC.AmountMax>=@Amount OR CBC.AmountMax=-1) AND (CBC.AmountMin<=@Amount OR CBC.AmountMin=-1) 
		  AND dbo.IsServiceDate(@Timezone, CBC.ServiceStartDate, CBC.ServiceEndDate) = 1

		-- 新增一般輪巡卡
		INSERT INTO @ProviderBankCardTemp (BankCardGUID, CardType, AmountMax, TotalCreditValueDay, TotalCreditValueWeek, TotalCreditValueMonth, CreditValueDay, CreditValueWeek, CreditValueMonth, LastDate, ProcessType, SortKey)
		SELECT CBC.BankCardGUID, CBC.CardType, CBC.AmountMax, CBC.TotalCreditValueDay, CBC.TotalCreditValueWeek, CBC.TotalCreditValueMonth, CBC.CreditValueDay, CBC.CreditValueWeek, CBC.CreditValueMonth, CBC.LastDate, 0, '1_' + CAST(newid() AS varchar(50))
		FROM ProviderBankCard AS CBC WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE CBC.forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND CBC.CardType IN (0, 1, 3) AND CBC.BankCardState=0 AND CBC.CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND CBC.Type=@Type AND (CBC.AmountMax>=@Amount OR CBC.AmountMax=-1) AND (CBC.AmountMin<=@Amount OR CBC.AmountMin=-1) 
		  AND dbo.IsServiceDate(@Timezone, CBC.ServiceStartDate, CBC.ServiceEndDate) = 1

		
		UPDATE @ProviderBankCardTemp SET ProcessType=1 WHERE  @DirectionType=0 AND
		(( CardType=0 AND BankCardGUID IN (SELECT BankCardGUID FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode))
		OR ( CardType=3 AND BankCardGUID IN (SELECT BankCardGUID FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND Amount=@Amount)))


		WHILE (1=1)
		BEGIN
			SET @_BankCardGUID = NULL
			SET @IsAvailCard = 0

			SELECT TOP 1
				   @_BankCardGUID=BankCardGUID,
				   @_BankCardType=CardType,
				   @TotalCreditValueDay=TotalCreditValueDay,
				   @TotalCreditValueWeek=TotalCreditValueWeek,
				   @TotalCreditValueMonth=TotalCreditValueMonth,
				   @CreditValueDay=CreditValueDay,
				   @CreditValueWeek=CreditValueWeek,
				   @CreditValueMonth=CreditValueMonth,
				   @LastDate=DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, LastDate)
			FROM @ProviderBankCardTemp 
			WHERE ProcessType=0
			ORDER BY SortKey

			--PRINT 'Check BankCard - ' + @_BankCardGUID

			IF (@_BankCardGUID IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				IF (CONVERT(varchar(20), @LastDate, 23) = CONVERT(varchar(20), @CurrentDate, 23))
				BEGIN
					-- 今天額度
					SET @FreeValueDay = @CreditValueDay
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					-- 已跨天
					SET @FreeValueDay = 0
				END

				IF (DATEPART(WEEK, @LastDate) = DATEPART(WEEK, @CurrentDate))
				BEGIN
					SET @FreeValueWeek = @CreditValueWeek
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @FreeValueWeek = 0
				END

				IF ((YEAR(@LastDate) = YEAR(@CurrentDate)) AND (MONTH(@LastDate) = MONTH(@CurrentDate)))
				BEGIN
					SET @FreeValueMonth = @CreditValueMonth
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @FreeValueMonth = 0
				END

				--PRINT 'Day=' + CAST(@FreeValueDay AS varchar(max)) + ', Week=' + CAST(@FreeValueWeek AS varchar(max)) + ', Month=' + CAST(@FreeValueMonth AS varchar(max))

				IF ((((@FreeValueDay + @Amount) <= @TotalCreditValueDay) OR (@TotalCreditValueDay = -1))
				   AND (((@FreeValueWeek + @Amount) <= @TotalCreditValueWeek) OR (@TotalCreditValueWeek = -1))
				   AND (((@FreeValueMonth + @Amount) <= @TotalCreditValueMonth) OR (@TotalCreditValueMonth = -1)))
				   SET @IsAvailCard = 1

				IF (@DirectionType = 1)
					SET @IsAvailCard = 1

				IF (@IsAvailCard = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @BankCardGUID = @_BankCardGUID
					SET @BankCardLockID = newid()
					SET @ExpireDate = DATEADD(ss, @LockTimeoutSecond, getutcdate())
					SET @RetValue = 0

					--PRINT 'Check Completed'
					INSERT INTO MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock  (BankCardLockID, BankCardGUID, forProviderCode, Amount, LastUsedDate, [ExpireDate]) 
					                           VALUES (@BankCardLockID, @BankCardGUID, @ProviderCode, @Amount, getutcdate(), @ExpireDate)

					UPDATE ProviderBankCard WITH (ROWLOCK) SET CreditValueDay=@FreeValueDay + @Amount, CreditValueWeek=@FreeValueWeek + @Amount, CreditValueMonth=@FreeValueMonth + @Amount, LastDate=getutcdate() WHERE BankCardGUID=@BankCardGUID

					BREAK
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					UPDATE @ProviderBankCardTemp SET ProcessType=1 WHERE BankCardGUID=@_BankCardGUID
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @RetValue = -2
				BREAK
			END

		END

		EXEC sp_releaseapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockOwner='Session'
		RETURN @RetValue
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spGetRandomValue]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spGetRandomValue]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@ExpireSeconds int,
	@RemarkText varchar(20) OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	-- return:
	-- 0 = 成功
	-- -1 = 鎖定失敗
	-- -2 = 無可用亂數

	DECLARE @RandomID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @RandomRemarkText varchar(20)
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @RetValue int

	SET @LockName = 'RandomTable'
	EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Session', @LockTimeout = 15000

	IF (@LockRet>=0)
	BEGIN
		-- Insert statements for procedure here
		SELECT TOP 1 @RandomID = RandomID, @RandomRemarkText = RemarkRandom
		FROM RandomTable WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE (LastUsed IS NULL) OR (DATEDIFF(ss, LastUsed, getdate())>@ExpireSeconds)
		ORDER BY RandomID

		IF (@RandomID IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE RandomTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET LastUsed = getdate() WHERE RandomID=@RandomID

			SET @RemarkText = @RandomRemarkText

			SET @RetValue = 0
		END
		ELSE
			SET @RetValue = -2

		EXEC sp_releaseapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockOwner='Session'
		RETURN @RetValue
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1

END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spModifyCompanyServicePointByWithdrawal]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spModifyCompanyServicePointByWithdrawal] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@WithdrawID int,
    @ServiceType varchar(20),
    @CompanyID  int,
	@CurrencyType varchar(20),
    @FinishtAmount decimal(18, 4)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- (需在外層增加 transaction)
	-- RetValue:
	--  0 = 成功
	-- -1 = 支付通道扣点失败
	-- -2 = 商户支付通道额度不足
	-- -3 = 商户钱包额度不足
	-- -4 = 商户钱包不存在
	-- -5 = 锁定失败
	-- -6 = 支付通道加点失败

	DECLARE @TmpFinishtAmount decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CompanyPointValue decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CompanyServicePointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	DECLARE @UpdateAmount decimal(18, 4) 
    DECLARE @PointRet INT 

	--通道总金额比笔数
	DECLARE @TotalAmount int
	DECLARE @TotalNum int
	DECLARE @PointDescription VARCHAR(200) 


	--遍歷交易使用
	DECLARE @TradeServiceType varchar(20)	
	DECLARE @TradeAmount decimal(22, 8)
	
	--結算統計日期

	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int

        BEGIN TRAN 
	   
	    SET @TmpFinishtAmount=@FinishtAmount

        SET @LockName = 'spModifyCompanyServicePointByWithdrawal' 
	   
        EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource=@LockName, 
             @LockMode='Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner='Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 180000 
        IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
		    BEGIN
			  --取得公司額度 
			  SELECT @CompanyPointValue = PointValue 
			  FROM   CompanyPoint WITH (NOLOCK) 
			  WHERE  forCompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType 
			  --取得支付通道額度
			  SELECT @CompanyServicePointValue = SystemPointValue 
			  FROM   CompanyServicePoint WITH (NOLOCK) 
			  WHERE  CompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType  AND ServiceType=@ServiceType	

			  --統計
		
			  SELECT @TotalAmount=SUM(SystemPointValue)
		      FROM CompanyServicePoint AS CSP
			  WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType AND ServiceType<>@ServiceType	
	
			  IF ( @CompanyPointValue IS NOT NULL ) 
			  BEGIN 
					IF ( @CompanyPointValue - @FinishtAmount >= 0 ) 
					BEGIN 
					    SET @TmpFinishtAmount=@TmpFinishtAmount-@CompanyServicePointValue
					    SET @FinishtAmount=@FinishtAmount-@CompanyServicePointValue
						IF ( @TotalAmount - @FinishtAmount >= 0 ) 
							BEGIN
								WHILE @FinishtAmount <> 0  --必须将金额扣至0
					     			BEGIN
										SELECT	TOP(1)
										@TradeServiceType=CSP.ServiceType,
										@TradeAmount=CSP.SystemPointValue 
										FROM CompanyServicePoint AS CSP
										WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType AND ServiceType<>@ServiceType	AND SystemPointValue<>0
										ORDER BY SystemPointValue 

										IF (@FinishtAmount>@TradeAmount)
										BEGIN
										 SET @UpdateAmount=@TradeAmount*-1
										 SET @FinishtAmount=@FinishtAmount-@TradeAmount
										END
										ELSE
										BEGIN
											SET @UpdateAmount=@FinishtAmount*-1
											SET @FinishtAmount=0
										END

										SET @PointDescription = 'spAddCompanyServicePoint,WithdrawID=' + CONVERT(varchar, @WithdrawID)
										 --加扣點 
										EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyServicePoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 4, @UpdateAmount, @WithdrawID, @PointDescription, @TradeServiceType 
								
										IF (@PointRet <> 0)
										BEGIN
											ROLLBACK	
											RETURN -1
										END
								    END

									EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyServicePoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 4, @TmpFinishtAmount, @WithdrawID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType 
								
									IF (@PointRet <> 0)
									BEGIN
										ROLLBACK	
										RETURN -6
									END
							END
							ELSE
							BEGIN
								ROLLBACK 	
								RETURN -2
							END
					END 
					ELSE 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK
						RETURN -3
					END 
				END 
			  ELSE 
			  BEGIN
					ROLLBACK 	
					RETURN -4
				END 

			  COMMIT
			  RETURN 0
			END

		ELSE
		BEGIN
			RETURN -5
		END

END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spModifyProviderPoint]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
