-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spModifyProviderPoint] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255)
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=營運商錢包有誤 
      -- -5=營運商餘額不足
      -- -6=加扣點失敗 
	  -- -7=該單非處在可充值之狀態

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 
      DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(255) 
	  --专属供应商
	  DECLARE @ProxyProviderCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @GroupID int
	  DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @GroupID2 int
	  DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint2 decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 
	  --營運商額度
      DECLARE @CompanyPointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(上游)
	  DECLARE @FinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @FinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	    --提現額度(代理上游)
	  DECLARE @ProxyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  --提現額度(下游)
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  DECLARE @APIDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	    --毛利
	  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  --提現單類型
	  DECLARE @WithdrawType INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
      --0=後台申請提現單=>後台審核/1=API申請代付=>後台審核/2=API申請代付=>不經後台審核
	  DECLARE @FloatType INT 
	  --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime

	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
             @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
             @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
			 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			 @WithdrawType = WithdrawType,
			 @APIDidAmount = Amount,
			 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
			 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
			 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
			 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
			 @FloatType=FloatType,
			 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
			 @CreateDate=CreateDate
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  --查询商户备注
	  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
	  FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK) 
   	  WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID 

	  --取得统计日其
	  SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	  SELECT @ProxyProviderCharge = WithdrawalCharge,
	         @GroupID=GroupID
	  FROM ProxyProviderOrder WITH (NOLOCK) 
	  WHERE forOrderSerial=@WithdrawSerial		

	  SELECT  @GroupID2=GroupID
	  FROM ProxyProviderPointHistory PPPH 
	  WHERE PPPH.TransactionID=@WithdrawID AND PPPH.OperatorType=1

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 3000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 

		--取得专属供应商群组额度(1)
		SELECT @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
		FROM   ProxyProviderGroup WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  GroupID = @GroupID AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 

		--取得专属供应商群组额度(2)
		SELECT @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint2 = CanUsePoint 
		FROM   ProxyProviderGroup WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  GroupID = @GroupID2 AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
		--取得专属供应商额度
		SELECT @ProxyCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
		FROM   ProxyProvider WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
	   --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawType IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@GroupID<>@GroupID2) 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		IF (@WithdrawType = 0)
	  		BEGIN
	  			SET @FinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CostCharge
	  			SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CollectCharge
	  	    END
	  		ELSE IF (@WithdrawType=1)
	  		BEGIN
	  			SET @FinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CostCharge
	  			SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CollectCharge
	  		END
	  		ELSE
	  		BEGIN
	  			ROLLBACK	
	  			RETURN -2		
	  		END	
	  	  END	
	  	  ELSE 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		ROLLBACK	
	  		RETURN -7	
	  	  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END

	   	SET @PointDescription = 'ReviewWithdrawal:WithdrawSerial=' + @WithdrawSerial 
		SET @ProxyFinishDidAmount=@WithdrawAmount+@ProxyProviderCharge

		--更新专属供应商群组额度(扣钱)
		UPDATE ProxyProviderGroup WITH (ROWLOCK) 
		SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint +@ProxyProviderCharge,
		CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint -@ProxyFinishDidAmount
		WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode And GroupID=@GroupID

		-- 更新专属供應商每日統計(扣钱)
		UPDATE ProxySummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
		SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + @WithdrawAmount,
		SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @ProxyProviderCharge,
			WithdrawalCount=WithdrawalCount+1					
		WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
			ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND 
			GroupID=@GroupID 

		IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
		BEGIN
		INSERT INTO ProxySummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,GroupID,WithdrawalCount)
		VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @WithdrawAmount, @ProxyProviderCharge,@GroupID,1)
		END

		
		--新增专属供应商流水资料
			INSERT INTO ProxyProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue,GroupValue,GroupBeforeValue, Description, TransactionID,GroupID)
			SELECT                             @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 1, @ProxyFinishDidAmount*-1, @ProxyCanUsePoint,@ProxyFinishDidAmount*-1, @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint,@Description, @WithdrawID,@GroupID
		
	
		--更新专属供应商群组额度(加钱)
		UPDATE ProxyProviderGroup WITH (ROWLOCK) 
		SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint -@ProxyProviderCharge,
		CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint +@ProxyFinishDidAmount
		WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode And GroupID=@GroupID2

		UPDATE ProxySummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
		SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount - @WithdrawAmount,
		SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount - @ProxyProviderCharge,
			WithdrawalCount=WithdrawalCount-1					
		WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
			ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND 
			GroupID=@GroupID2 
						 
		IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
		BEGIN
		INSERT INTO ProxySummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,GroupID,WithdrawalCount)
		VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @WithdrawAmount*-1, @ProxyProviderCharge*-1,@GroupID2,0)
		END

		--新增专属供应商流水资料
			INSERT INTO ProxyProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue,GroupValue,GroupBeforeValue, Description, TransactionID,GroupID)
			SELECT                             @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 1, @ProxyFinishDidAmount, @ProxyCanUsePoint,@ProxyFinishDidAmount, @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint2,@Description, @WithdrawID,@GroupID2
		
		COMMIT 
		RETURN 0 

      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spPaymentBindingBankCard]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spPaymentBindingBankCard]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@PaymentID int,
	@DirectionType int,    -- 0=買入/1=賣出
	@Amount decimal(22, 8),  -- 細節訂單金額(佔主訂單額度)
	@ActualAmount decimal(22, 8),  -- 細節訂單實際收款額度, 區塊鏈使用幣值
	@UserBankCode varchar(20),
	@UserBankName nvarchar(50),
	@UserBranchName nvarchar(20),
	@UserBankNumber varchar(50),
	@UserAccountName nvarchar(50),
	@UserBankProvince nvarchar(50),
	@UserBankCity nvarchar(50),
	@UserIdentity nvarchar(50),
	@Type int    -- 0=銀行卡/1=支付寶
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=Success
	-- -1 = 鎖定失敗
	-- -2 = PaymentID 不存在
	-- -3 = 狀態錯誤
	-- -4 = 無可用卡片或錢包
	-- -5 = 亂數表用盡
	-- -6 = 已超過訂單總金額
	-- -99 = 未知錯誤

	DECLARE @ProcessStatus int
	DECLARE @ExpireDate datetime
	DECLARE @CompanyID int
	DECLARE @AvailRet int
	DECLARE @StoreID int
	DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
	DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
	DECLARE @FreeAmount decimal(22, 8)
	DECLARE @TotalAmount decimal(22, 8)
	DECLARE @BankCardTimeoutSecond int
	DECLARE @WalletTimeoutSecond int
	DECLARE @BankCardLockID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @BankCardGUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @CompanyWalletGUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @RetValue int
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @TransferRemarkExpireSecond int
	DECLARE @TransferRemark varchar(20)
	DECLARE @TransferRemarkRet int

	-- 取得附言
	SET @TransferRemarkExpireSecond = (86400 * 4)
	EXEC @TransferRemarkRet = spGetRandomValue @TransferRemarkExpireSecond, @TransferRemark OUTPUT
	IF (@TransferRemarkRet = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @LockName = 'Payment'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Session', @LockTimeout = 15000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT @ProcessStatus=ProcessStatus,
				   @CompanyID=forCompanyID,
				   @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
				   @CurrencyType=CurrencyType,
				   @TotalAmount=OrderAmount,
				   @FreeAmount=(OrderAmount - DetailAmount)
			FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK)
			WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID

	        SELECT @WalletTimeoutSecond = WalletTimeoutSecond,
	               @BankCardTimeoutSecond = BankCardTimeoutSecond
	        FROM ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK)
	        WHERE ProviderCode=@ProviderCode

			IF ((@FreeAmount - @Amount) >= 0)
			BEGIN
				IF (@ProcessStatus IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					IF (@ProcessStatus = -1 OR @ProcessStatus = 0)
					BEGIN
						-- Status 只能接受 -1 或 0
						EXEC @AvailRet = spGetAvailableBankCard_Internal @ProviderCode,@CompanyID,@UserIdentity, @DirectionType, @CurrencyType, @Amount, @BankCardTimeoutSecond, @Type, @BankCardLockID output, @BankCardGUID output, @ExpireDate output

						IF (@AvailRet = 0)
						BEGIN
							INSERT INTO PaymentDetailBankCard (forPaymentID, forCompanyID, forProviderCode, DirectionType, DetailStatus, CurrencyType, forBankCardGUID, forBankCardLockID, UserBankCode, UserBankName, UserBranchName, UserBankNumber, UserAccountName, UserBankProvince, UserBankCity, Amount, ActualAmount, TransferRemark, ExpireDate, LastUpdate) 
							                           VALUES (@PaymentID, @CompanyID, @ProviderCode, @DirectionType, 2, @CurrencyType, @BankCardGUID, @BankCardLockID, @UserBankCode, @UserBankName, @UserBranchName, @UserBankNumber, @UserAccountName, @UserBankProvince, @UserBankCity, @Amount, @ActualAmount, @TransferRemark, @ExpireDate, getdate())

							IF ((@FreeAmount - @Amount) = 0)
								UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=1, DetailAmount+=@Amount WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID AND (ProcessStatus=-1 OR ProcessStatus=0)
							ELSE
								UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET DetailAmount+=@Amount WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID AND (ProcessStatus=-1 OR ProcessStatus=0)

							SET @RetValue = 0
						END
						ELSE IF (@AvailRet = -1)
							SET @RetValue = -1
						ELSE IF (@AvailRet = -2)
							SET @RetValue = -4
						ELSE
							SET @RetValue = -99
					END
					ELSE
						SET @RetValue = -3
				END
				ELSE
					SET @RetValue = -2
			END
			ELSE
				SET @RetValue = -6


			EXEC sp_releaseapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockOwner='Session'
			RETURN @RetValue
		END
		ELSE
			RETURN -1
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -5

END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spReleaseBankCard_Internal]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReleaseBankCard_Internal]
	@BankCardLockID varchar(50)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @BankCardGUID varchar(50)
	DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
	DECLARE @LastUsedDate datetime
    DECLARE @LastUsedDateUTC datetime
	DECLARE @CardLastDate datetime
	DECLARE @Amount decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CurrentDate datetime
	DECLARE @ExecString nvarchar(MAX)
	DECLARE @ExecWhereString nvarchar(MAX)

	SELECT @BankCardGUID=BankCardGUID,
	       @ProviderCode=forProviderCode,
		   @LastUsedDateUTC=LastUsedDate,
		   @Amount=Amount
	FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WITH (NOLOCK)
	WHERE BankCardLockID=@BankCardLockID

	IF (@BankCardGUID IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
		FROM ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE @ProviderCode=@ProviderCode

		SELECT @CardLastDate=DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, LastDate)
		FROM ProviderBankCard WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE BankCardGUID=@BankCardGUID

		SET @LastUsedDate=DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, @LastUsedDateUTC)
		SET @CurrentDate=DATEADD(HOUR, @Timezone, getutcdate())
		SET @ExecString = ''
		SET @ExecWhereString = ''

		IF (CONVERT(varchar(20), @LastUsedDate, 23) <> CONVERT(varchar(20), @CurrentDate, 23))
		BEGIN
			-- 取得銀行卡的日期與完成日期不同, 已跨日, 需要扣掉跨日額度
			IF (@ExecWhereString <> '')
				SET @ExecWhereString += ','

			IF (CONVERT(varchar(20), @CardLastDate, 23) = CONVERT(varchar(20), @CurrentDate, 23))
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueDay += @_Amount'
			ELSE
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueDay = @_Amount'
		END

		IF (DATEPART(WEEK, @LastUsedDate) <> DATEPART(WEEK, @CurrentDate))
		BEGIN
			-- 不是同一周
			IF (@ExecWhereString <> '')
				SET @ExecWhereString += ','

			IF (DATEPART(WEEK, @CardLastDate) = DATEPART(WEEK, @CurrentDate))
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueWeek += @_Amount'
			ELSE
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueWeek = @_Amount'
		END

		IF ((YEAR(@LastUsedDate) <> YEAR(@CurrentDate)) OR (MONTH(@LastUsedDate) <> MONTH(@CurrentDate)))
		BEGIN
			-- 不是同一月
			IF (@ExecWhereString <> '')
				SET @ExecWhereString += ','

			IF ((YEAR(@LastUsedDate) = YEAR(@CurrentDate)) AND (MONTH(@LastUsedDate) = MONTH(@CurrentDate)))
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueMonth += @_Amount'
			ELSE
				SET @ExecWhereString += 'CreditValueMonth = @_Amount'
		END

		IF (@ExecWhereString <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @ExecString = 'UPDATE ProviderBankCard WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ' + @ExecWhereString + ',LastDate=getutcdate() WHERE BankCardGUID=@_BankCardGUID'

			EXEC sp_executesql @ExecString, N'@_Amount decimal(18, 4),@_BankCardGUID varchar(50)', @_Amount = @Amount, @_BankCardGUID = @BankCardGUID
		END

		-- Insert statements for procedure here
		DELETE FROM MemOpt_ProviderBankCardLock WITH (SNAPSHOT) WHERE BankCardLockID=@BankCardLockID
	END

	RETURN 0
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spReviewProviderWithdrawal]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewProviderWithdrawal] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255)
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=營運商錢包有誤 
      -- -5=營運商餘額不足
      -- -6=加扣點失敗 
	  -- -7=該單非處在可充值之狀態

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 
      DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(255) 
	  --专属供应商
	  DECLARE @ProxyProviderCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @GroupID int
	  DECLARE @AdminGroupID int
	  DECLARE @HandleByAdminID int
	  DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	  --是否扣除毛利
	  DECLARE @DeductionProfit int  
	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 
	  --營運商額度
      DECLARE @CompanyPointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(上游)
	  DECLARE @FinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @FinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	    --提現額度(代理上游)
	  DECLARE @ProxyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  --提現額度(下游)
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  DECLARE @APIDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	    --毛利
	  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  --提現單類型
	  DECLARE @WithdrawType INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
      --0=後台申請提現單=>後台審核/1=API申請代付=>後台審核/2=API申請代付=>不經後台審核
	  DECLARE @FloatType INT 
	  --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
             @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
             @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
			 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			 @WithdrawType = WithdrawType,
			 @APIDidAmount = Amount,
			 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
			 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
			 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
			 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
			 @FloatType=FloatType,
			 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
			 @CreateDate=CreateDate,
			 @HandleByAdminID=HandleByAdminID
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  SELECT @AdminGroupID=GroupID
	  FROM   AdminTable WITH (NOLOCK) 
	  WHERE  AdminID=@HandleByAdminID	

	  --查询商户备注
      SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
      FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

	  --取得统计日其
	  SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	  SELECT @ProxyProviderCharge = WithdrawalCharge,
	         @GroupID=GroupID,
			 @DeductionProfit=DeductionProfit
	  FROM ProxyProviderOrder WITH (NOLOCK) 
	  WHERE forOrderSerial=@WithdrawSerial And Type=1			

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
		--取得公司額度 
		SELECT @CompanyPointValue = PointValue 
		FROM   CompanyPoint WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forCompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType 
		--取得专属供应商群组额度
		SELECT @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
		FROM   ProxyProviderGroup WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  GroupID = @AdminGroupID AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
		--取得专属供应商额度
		SELECT @ProxyCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
		FROM   ProxyProvider WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
	   --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawType IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 1) 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		IF (@WithdrawType = 0)
	  		BEGIN
	  			SET @FinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CostCharge
	  			SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CollectCharge
	  	    END
	  		ELSE IF (@WithdrawType=1)
	  		BEGIN
	  			SET @FinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CostCharge
	  			SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CollectCharge
	  		END
	  		ELSE
	  		BEGIN
	  			ROLLBACK	
	  			RETURN -2		
	  		END	
	  	  END	
		  ELSE IF(@WithdrawStatus = 2 OR @WithdrawStatus = 3) 	
		  BEGIN
		    COMMIT	
	  		RETURN 0	
		  END	
	  	  ELSE 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		ROLLBACK	
	  		RETURN -7	
	  	  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END

  	    IF ( @CompanyPointValue IS NOT NULL ) 
		BEGIN 
			IF ( @CompanyPointValue - @FinishtAmount >= 0 ) 
			BEGIN 
					
				IF ( @ProxyProviderCharge IS NULL) 
				BEGIN
					SET @ProxyProviderCharge=0
				END
	
				SET @PointDescription = 'ReviewWithdrawal:WithdrawSerial=' + @WithdrawSerial 
				SET @FinishDidAmount = @FinishtAmount * -1
				SET @CompanyFinishDidAmount=@CompanyFinishtAmount * -1
				SET @ProfitAmount=@CollectCharge-@CostCharge
				SET @ProxyFinishDidAmount=(@WithdrawAmount+@ProxyProviderCharge)*-1
			    --加扣點 
			    EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 0, @CompanyFinishDidAmount, @WithdrawID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType 

			    IF ( @PointRet = 0 ) 
			    BEGIN
				    
					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 2, @FinishDidAmount,@ProfitAmount,@WithdrawID, @PointDescription
					
					IF ( @PointRet <> 0 ) 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -8
					END

					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 2, FinishDate=GETDATE(), FinishAmount = @CompanyFinishtAmount
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge),
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge						
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge), @CostCharge)
					END

					
					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CollectCharge,
						SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge)	
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryWithdrawalAmount)
					                            	VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@CollectCharge,@CostCharge,@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge)
					END

					-- 更新代理供应商钱包金额与代理供应商可用额度
					UPDATE ProxyProvider WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint +@ProxyProviderCharge,
					    CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint +@ProxyFinishDidAmount
					WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
					--更新专属供应商群组额度
					IF ( @DeductionProfit=1 ) 
					BEGIN
						UPDATE ProxyProviderGroup WITH (ROWLOCK) 
						SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint +@ProxyProviderCharge,
						CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint +@ProxyFinishDidAmount,
						ProfitAmount=ProfitAmount+@ProfitAmount
						WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode And GroupID=@AdminGroupID
						END
					ELSE 
					BEGIN 
						UPDATE ProxyProviderGroup WITH (ROWLOCK) 
						SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint +@ProxyProviderCharge,
						CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint +@ProxyFinishDidAmount,
						ProfitAmount=ProfitAmount+@ProfitAmount+@FinishDidAmount
						WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode And GroupID=@AdminGroupID
					END 

					-- 更新专属供應商每日統計
					UPDATE ProxySummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + @WithdrawAmount,
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @ProxyProviderCharge,
						 WithdrawalCount=WithdrawalCount+1					
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND 
                          GroupID=@AdminGroupID 
						 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO ProxySummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,GroupID,WithdrawalCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @WithdrawAmount, @ProxyProviderCharge,@AdminGroupID,1)
					END

					--新增专属供应商流水资料
							INSERT INTO ProxyProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue,GroupValue,GroupBeforeValue, Description, TransactionID,GroupID)
			             	SELECT                             @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 1, @ProxyFinishDidAmount, @ProxyCanUsePoint,@ProxyFinishDidAmount, @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint,@Description, @WithdrawID,@AdminGroupID
		
		           --修改订单记录群组
				   IF(@AdminGroupID<>@GroupID)
				   BEGIN
					 UPDATE ProxyProviderOrder WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					 SET GroupID= @AdminGroupID
					 WHERE forOrderSerial=@WithdrawSerial And Type=1
				   END
			       COMMIT 
				   RETURN 0 
			    END 
				ELSE 
			    BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK 

			        UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Description = @Description + N'加扣點失敗',ManagerRejectDescription='加扣點失敗'
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

			        RETURN -6
			    END 
			END 
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
				SET    Description = @Description + N'餘額不足',ManagerRejectDescription='餘額不足'
				WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			    
				RETURN -5
			END 
		END 
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK 

			UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			SET    Description = @Description + N'公司額度有誤',ManagerRejectDescription='公司額度有誤'
			WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			RETURN -4
		END 
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
	    ROLLBACK
		
		IF (@LockRet=-1)
		RETURN -91
		IF (@LockRet=-2)
		RETURN -92
		IF (@LockRet=-3)
		RETURN -93
		IF (@LockRet=-999)
		RETURN -94
			
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawal]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawal] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255)
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=營運商錢包有誤 
      -- -5=營運商餘額不足
      -- -6=加扣點失敗 
	  -- -7=該單非處在可充值之狀態
	  -- -8=區塊鏈交易所費率未設定
	  -- -9=供應商浮動馬數未設定
	  -- -10=商戶浮動馬數未設定

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 
      DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(255) 
	  DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
      DECLARE @InsideLevel int
	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 
	  --營運商額度
      DECLARE @CompanyPointValue decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(上游)
	  DECLARE @FinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @FinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  --提現額度(下游)
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishtAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @CompanyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 

	  DECLARE @APIDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @DownRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @UpRate decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT
	  
	    --毛利
	  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  --提現單類型
	  DECLARE @WithdrawType INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
      --0=後台申請提現單=>後台審核/1=API申請代付=>後台審核/2=API申請代付=>不經後台審核
	  DECLARE @FloatType INT 
	  --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	  --訂單類型 0=銀行卡/1=區塊鏈
	  DECLARE @IsBlockChainOrder INT
	  --區塊鏈交易所費率
	  DECLARE @BlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @UpBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --下游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @DownBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)

	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

	  DECLARE @WithdrawLimitType INT
	  DECLARE @AgentServiceType varchar(20) 

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
	      --取得提現單資料 
		  SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
				 @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
				 @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
				 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
				 @WithdrawType = WithdrawType,
				 @APIDidAmount = UpDidAmount,
				 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
				 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
				 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
				 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
				 @FloatType=FloatType,
				 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
				 @CreateDate=CreateDate,
				 @IsBlockChainOrder=IsBlockChainOrder,
				 @UpBlockChainRate=UpBlockChainRate,
				 @DownBlockChainRate=DownBlockChainRate,
				 @DownRate=DownRate,
				 @UpRate=UpRate
		  FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
		  WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

		  --查询商户备注
 		  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone,
				 @InsideLevel = InsideLevel,
				 @SortKey = SortKey
 		  FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
    		  WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

          --設定 WithdrawLimitType 1=後台下發 2= API 代付
		  IF @FloatType = 0 
		   BEGIN
			   SET @WithdrawLimitType = 1 
			   SET @AgentServiceType = @ServiceType
		   END
		  ELSE 
			BEGIN
				SET @WithdrawLimitType = 2 
				SET @AgentServiceType = ''
		   	END

        --取得统计日其
		SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

		--取得手續費率
		SELECT @DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces
		FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode
		--取得公司額度 
		SELECT @CompanyPointValue = PointValue 
		FROM   CompanyPoint WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forCompanyID = @CompanyID AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType 
	
	    IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
		BEGIN
			--取得區塊鏈費率與浮動費率
			SELECT @BlockChainRate = Rate
			FROM   BlockChainRate WITH (NOLOCK) 
			WHERE  CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

			IF( @BlockChainRate IS NULL OR @BlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -8
			END

			IF( @UpBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @UpBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -9
			END

			IF( @DownBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @DownBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -10
			END
		END

	   --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawType IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 1) 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		IF (@WithdrawType = 0)
	  		BEGIN
			    IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
				    SET @FinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CostCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
	  				SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CollectCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN 
					SET @FinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CostCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
	  				SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @WithdrawAmount + @CollectCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END
	  			
	  	    END
	  		ELSE IF (@WithdrawType=1)
	  		BEGIN
			    IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
					SET @FinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CostCharge+floor(@APIDidAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
	  				SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CollectCharge+floor(@APIDidAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN 
				    SET @FinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CostCharge+(@APIDidAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
	  			    SET @CompanyFinishtAmount = @APIDidAmount + @CollectCharge+(@APIDidAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END
	  		END
	  		ELSE
	  		BEGIN
	  			ROLLBACK	
	  			RETURN -2		
	  		END	
	  	  END	
		  ELSE IF(@WithdrawStatus = 2 OR @WithdrawStatus = 3) 	
		  BEGIN
		    COMMIT	
	  		RETURN 0	
		  END	
	  	  ELSE 
	  	  BEGIN
	  		ROLLBACK	
	  		RETURN -7	
	  	  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END

  	    IF ( @CompanyPointValue IS NOT NULL ) 
		BEGIN 
			IF ( @CompanyPointValue - @CompanyFinishtAmount >= 0 ) 
			BEGIN 
				SET @PointDescription = 'ReviewWithdrawal:WithdrawSerial=' + @WithdrawSerial 
				SET @FinishDidAmount = @FinishtAmount * -1
				SET @CompanyFinishDidAmount=@CompanyFinishtAmount * -1
				SET @ProfitAmount=@CollectCharge-@CostCharge
			    --加扣點 
			    EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 0, @CompanyFinishDidAmount, @WithdrawID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType 

			    IF ( @PointRet = 0 ) 
			    BEGIN
				    
					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 2, @FinishDidAmount,@ProfitAmount,@WithdrawID, @PointDescription
					
					IF ( @PointRet <> 0 ) 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -8
					END

					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 2, FinishDate=GETDATE(), FinishAmount = @CompanyFinishtAmount,FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge),
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount + 1	
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge), @CostCharge,1)
					END

					
					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CollectCharge,
						SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + (@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge),
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount + 1	
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode

					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryWithdrawalAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
					                            	VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@CollectCharge,@CostCharge,@FinishtAmount-@CostCharge,1)
					END

					IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
						BEGIN
						-- 更新區塊鏈每日統計
						UPDATE SummaryBlockChainByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
						--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
						--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
						SET SummaryCompanyAmount=SummaryCompanyAmount + @CompanyFinishtAmount,
							SummaryProviderAmount=SummaryProviderAmount + @FinishtAmount,
							UpSummaryBlockChainAmount=UpSummaryBlockChainAmount + ROUND(@FinishtAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0),
							DownSummaryBlockChainAmount=DownSummaryBlockChainAmount + ROUND(@CompanyFinishtAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0)
						WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
								CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
								ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
								forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
								forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND
								DownBlockChainRate=@DownBlockChainRate AND
								UpBlockChainRate=@UpBlockChainRate AND
								BlockChainRate=@BlockChainRate

						IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
						BEGIN
							INSERT INTO SummaryBlockChainByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,DownBlockChainRate,UpBlockChainRate,BlockChainRate,SummaryCompanyAmount,SummaryProviderAmount,UpSummaryBlockChainAmount,DownSummaryBlockChainAmount)
					                            		VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@DownBlockChainRate,@UpBlockChainRate,@BlockChainRate,@CompanyFinishtAmount,@FinishtAmount,ROUND(@FinishtAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0), ROUND(@CompanyFinishtAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0))
						END
					END

						--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel > 0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE (
						Temp_Index int,
						ChildCompanyID int,
						CompanyID int,
						ReceiveValue decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectCharge decimal(18, 4),
						CollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
						)

						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
						(SELECT
							ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
							C.CompanyID,
							C.ParentCompanyID,
							(CASE C.ParentCompanyID
							WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.Rate + CS1.Charge
							ELSE (@WithdrawAmount * 0.01 * (CS1.Rate - CS2.Rate) + CS1.Charge - CS2.Charge)
							END),
							CS1.Rate,
							CS1.Charge,
							CS2.Rate,
							CS2.Charge
						FROM CompanyTable AS C
						LEFT JOIN WithdrawLimit AS CS1
							ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID
							AND CS1.ServiceType = @AgentServiceType
							AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND CS1.WithdrawLimitType = @WithdrawLimitType
							LEFT JOIN WithdrawLimit AS CS2
							ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID
							AND CS2.ServiceType = @AgentServiceType
							AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND CS2.WithdrawLimitType = @WithdrawLimitType
						WHERE C.SortKey <> ''
						AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey + '%'
						)


						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING (SELECT
						T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
						ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue, 0) AS ReceiveValue,
						ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue, 0) AS PayValue
						FROM @TempAgent AS T1
						RIGHT JOIN @TempAgent AS T2
						ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID) AS Accounting
						ON (SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
						)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
						--該下發給該代理的淨額
						UPDATE SET
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
						INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount, forProviderCode)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate,
						@CurrencyType,
						@ServiceType,
						Accounting.CompanyID,
						0,
						0,
						Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						Accounting.PayValue,
						@ProviderCode
						);

						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
						ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate,OrderType)
						SELECT
							@CurrencyType,
							@ServiceType,
							T.CompanyID,
							T.CollectRate,
							T.CollectCharge,
							T.ChildCompanyID,
							T.ChildCollectRate,
							T.ChildCollectCharge,
							T.ReceiveValue,
							@WithdrawID,
							GETDATE(),
							1
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0


					END

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0

			       COMMIT 
				   RETURN 0 
			    END 
				ELSE 
			    BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK 

			        UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Description = @Description + '加扣點失敗',ManagerRejectDescription='加扣點失敗'
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

			        RETURN -6
			    END 
			END 
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
				SET    Description = @Description + '餘額不足',ManagerRejectDescription='餘額不足'
				WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			    
				RETURN -5
			END 
		END 
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK 

			UPDATE withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			SET    Description = @Description + '公司額度有誤',ManagerRejectDescription='公司額度有誤'
			WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial
			RETURN -4
		END 
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
	    ROLLBACK	
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawalFailtoSuccess]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawalFailtoSuccess] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255),
  @forAdminID int
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=修改供应商钱包失败 
      -- -5=修改商户钱包失败

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 

	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 

	  DECLARE @ProviderWithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @CompanyWithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT

	  --是否扣除毛利
	  DECLARE @DeductionProfit int  
	  DECLARE @WithdrawRate decimal(18, 4)
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
	   --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	  --訂單類型 0=銀行卡/1=區塊鏈
	  DECLARE @IsBlockChainOrder INT
	  --區塊鏈交易所費率
	  DECLARE @BlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @UpBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --下游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @DownBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)

	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
             @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
             @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
			 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
			 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
			 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
			 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
			 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
		     @CreateDate=CreateDate,
			 @IsBlockChainOrder=IsBlockChainOrder,
		 	 @UpBlockChainRate=UpBlockChainRate,
			 @DownBlockChainRate=DownBlockChainRate
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
		BEGIN
			--取得區塊鏈費率與浮動費率
			SELECT @BlockChainRate = Rate
			FROM   BlockChainRate WITH (NOLOCK) 
			WHERE  CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

			IF( @BlockChainRate IS NULL OR @BlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -8
			END

			IF( @UpBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @UpBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -9
			END

			IF( @DownBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @DownBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -10
			END
		END

	  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
 	  FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID


	  --取得统计日其
	  SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	  --取得供应商资料
      SELECT @WithdrawRate = WithdrawRate ,@DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces
      FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
			  --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawID IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 3) 
	 		BEGIN 
				IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
				  SET @ProviderWithdrawAmount=(@WithdrawAmount +@CostCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@WithdrawRate*0.01))*-1
				  SET @CompanyWithdrawAmount=(@WithdrawAmount+@CollectCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@WithdrawRate*0.01))*-1
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
				  SET @ProviderWithdrawAmount=(@WithdrawAmount +@CostCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@WithdrawRate*0.01))*-1
				  SET @CompanyWithdrawAmount=(@WithdrawAmount+@CollectCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@WithdrawRate*0.01))*-1
				END

			    --修改供应商钱包数据
			    EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderManual 1,@ProviderCode ,@CurrencyType, @ProviderWithdrawAmount, N'失败转成功单', @WithdrawSerial, @forAdminID

			    IF ( @PointRet = 0 ) 
			    BEGIN
					---- 更新营运商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyManual 1,@ServiceType ,@CurrencyType, @CompanyWithdrawAmount, N'失败转成功单',@WithdrawSerial,@CompanyID, @forAdminID
					
					IF ( @PointRet <> 0 ) 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -5
					END

					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 2, FinishDate=GETDATE(), FinishAmount = @CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(GETDATE(),@Timezone)
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + @WithdrawAmount,
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount + 1
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @WithdrawAmount, @CostCharge,1)
					END

					
					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CollectCharge,
						SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount + @CostCharge,
						SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount + @WithdrawAmount,
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount + 1
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryWithdrawalAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
					                            	VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@CollectCharge,@CostCharge,@WithdrawAmount,1)
					END

					IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
						BEGIN
						-- 更新區塊鏈每日統計
						UPDATE SummaryBlockChainByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
						SET SummaryCompanyAmount=SummaryCompanyAmount + @CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1,
							SummaryProviderAmount=SummaryProviderAmount + @ProviderWithdrawAmount*-1,
							UpSummaryBlockChainAmount=UpSummaryBlockChainAmount + ROUND((@ProviderWithdrawAmount*-1) / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0),
							DownSummaryBlockChainAmount=DownSummaryBlockChainAmount + ROUND((@CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1) / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0)
						WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
								CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
								ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
								forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
								forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND
								DownBlockChainRate=@DownBlockChainRate AND
								UpBlockChainRate=@UpBlockChainRate AND
								BlockChainRate=@BlockChainRate

						IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
						BEGIN
							INSERT INTO SummaryBlockChainByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,DownBlockChainRate,UpBlockChainRate,BlockChainRate,SummaryCompanyAmount,SummaryProviderAmount,UpSummaryBlockChainAmount,DownSummaryBlockChainAmount)
					                            		VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@DownBlockChainRate,@UpBlockChainRate,@BlockChainRate,@CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1,@ProviderWithdrawAmount*-1,ROUND((@ProviderWithdrawAmount*-1) / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0), ROUND((@CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1) / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0))
						END
					END

			       COMMIT 
				   RETURN 0 
			    END 
				ELSE 
			    BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK 
			        RETURN -4
			    END 
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawalProccessingtoDefault] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawID INT
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 

	  DECLARE @WithdrawSerial varchar(50)  
	  DECLARE @BeforeStatus INT
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 

      BEGIN TRAN 

	  --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @WithdrawSerial = WithdrawSerial,
	         @BeforeStatus=Status
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  @WithdrawID = WithdrawID

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
			  --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawSerial IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN
	  	  IF(@BeforeStatus = 1) 
	 		BEGIN 
			    UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) SET Status=0,UpStatus=0 WHERE WithdrawID=@WithdrawID	  
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
				END
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO

-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawaltoFail] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawSerial varchar(255),
  @forAdminID int
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 
      -- -4=修改供应商钱包失败 
      -- -5=修改商户钱包失败

      DECLARE @CompanyID INT 
      DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20) 
	  DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
      DECLARE @InsideLevel int

	  --是否扣除毛利
	  DECLARE @DeductionProfit int  
      DECLARE @UpRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --加扣點回傳值
	  DECLARE @PointRet INT 

	  DECLARE @ProviderWithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @CompanyWithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @WithdrawAmount decimal(18, 4)
	  --跟下游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @DownRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游收取的手續費
	  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT
      --提現單ID 
      DECLARE @WithdrawID INT 
	  DECLARE @WithdrawStatus INT 
	  --備註
	  DECLARE @Description nvarchar(200)
	  
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 
	  --結算統計日期
	  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	　--訂單類型 0=銀行卡/1=區塊鏈
	  DECLARE @IsBlockChainOrder INT
	  --區塊鏈交易所費率
	  DECLARE @BlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --上游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @UpBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)
	  --下游區塊鏈浮動費率
	  DECLARE @DownBlockChainRate decimal(18, 4)

	  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	  DECLARE @WithdrawLimitType INT
	  DECLARE @FloatType INT 
	  DECLARE @AgentServiceType varchar(20) 

      --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @CompanyID = forCompanyID, 
             @CurrencyType = CurrencyType, 
             @WithdrawAmount = Amount,
			 @WithdrawID=WithdrawID,
			 @CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
			 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
			 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
			 @WithdrawStatus = Status,
			 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
		     @CreateDate=CreateDate,
			 @IsBlockChainOrder=IsBlockChainOrder,
		 	 @UpBlockChainRate=UpBlockChainRate,
			 @DownBlockChainRate=DownBlockChainRate,
			 @DownRate=DownRate,
			 @UpRate=UpRate,
			 @FloatType=FloatType
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

	  SELECT @Timezone=Timezone,
			@InsideLevel = InsideLevel,
			@SortKey = SortKey
 	  FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

	  --設定 WithdrawLimitType 1=後台下發 2= API 代付
		IF @FloatType = 0 
		BEGIN
			SET @WithdrawLimitType = 1 
			SET @AgentServiceType = @ServiceType
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			SET @WithdrawLimitType = 2 
			SET @AgentServiceType = ''
		END

	  IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
		BEGIN
			--取得區塊鏈費率與浮動費率
			SELECT @BlockChainRate = Rate
			FROM   BlockChainRate WITH (NOLOCK) 
			WHERE  CurrencyType = @CurrencyType

			IF( @BlockChainRate IS NULL OR @BlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -8
			END

			IF( @UpBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @UpBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -9
			END

			IF( @DownBlockChainRate IS NULL OR @DownBlockChainRate=0 ) 
			BEGIN
			  ROLLBACK	
	  	      RETURN -10
			END
	   END

	  --取得统计日其
	  SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

   	  --取得手續費率
	  SELECT @DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces
	  FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
	  WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

      BEGIN TRAN 

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
			  --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawID IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN

	  	  IF(@WithdrawStatus = 2) 
	 		BEGIN 
			    IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
						SET @ProviderWithdrawAmount=@WithdrawAmount +@CostCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
						SET @CompanyWithdrawAmount=@WithdrawAmount+@CollectCharge+floor(@WithdrawAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN 
						SET @ProviderWithdrawAmount=@WithdrawAmount +@CostCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@UpRate*0.01)
						SET @CompanyWithdrawAmount=@WithdrawAmount+@CollectCharge+(@WithdrawAmount*@DownRate*0.01)
				END

			    --修改供应商钱包数据
			    EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderManual 1,@ProviderCode ,@CurrencyType, @ProviderWithdrawAmount, N'成功转失败单', @WithdrawSerial, @forAdminID

			    IF ( @PointRet = 0 ) 
			    BEGIN
					---- 更新营运商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyManual 1,@ServiceType ,@CurrencyType, @CompanyWithdrawAmount, N'成功转失败单',@WithdrawSerial,@CompanyID, @forAdminID
					
					IF ( @PointRet <> 0 ) 
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -5
					END

					--更新交易單狀態 
			        UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			        SET    Status = 3, FinishDate=GETDATE(), FinishAmount = 0,FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
			        WHERE  WithdrawSerial = @WithdrawSerial

					
					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount - @WithdrawAmount,
						SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount - @CostCharge,
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount -1
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryWithdrawalAmount, SummaryWithdrawalChargeAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, (@WithdrawAmount*-1), (@CostCharge*-1),0)
					END

					
					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount - @CollectCharge,
						SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount=SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount - @CostCharge,
					    SummaryWithdrawalAmount=SummaryWithdrawalAmount - @WithdrawAmount,
						WithdrawalSuccessCount=WithdrawalSuccessCount -1
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,SummaryCompanyWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryProviderWithdrawalChargeAmount,SummaryWithdrawalAmount,WithdrawalSuccessCount)
					                            	VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,(@CollectCharge*-1),(@CostCharge*-1),(@WithdrawAmount*-1),0)
					END

					IF ( @IsBlockChainOrder = 1 ) 
						BEGIN
						-- 更新區塊鏈每日統計
						UPDATE SummaryBlockChainByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
						SET SummaryCompanyAmount=SummaryCompanyAmount - @CompanyWithdrawAmount,
							SummaryProviderAmount=SummaryProviderAmount - @ProviderWithdrawAmount,
						    UpSummaryBlockChainAmount=UpSummaryBlockChainAmount - ROUND(@ProviderWithdrawAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0),
							DownSummaryBlockChainAmount=DownSummaryBlockChainAmount - ROUND(@CompanyWithdrawAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0)
						WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
								CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
								ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
								forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
								forProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND
								DownBlockChainRate=@DownBlockChainRate AND
								UpBlockChainRate=@UpBlockChainRate AND
								BlockChainRate=@BlockChainRate

						IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
						BEGIN
							INSERT INTO SummaryBlockChainByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID,forProviderCode,DownBlockChainRate,UpBlockChainRate,BlockChainRate,SummaryCompanyAmount,SummaryProviderAmount,UpSummaryBlockChainAmount,DownSummaryBlockChainAmount)
					                            		VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,@DownBlockChainRate,@UpBlockChainRate,@BlockChainRate,@CompanyWithdrawAmount*-1,@ProviderWithdrawAmount*-1,ROUND(@ProviderWithdrawAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @UpBlockChainRate), 4, 0)*-1, ROUND(@CompanyWithdrawAmount / (@BlockChainRate + @DownBlockChainRate), 4, 0)*-1)
						END
					END

					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel > 0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE (
						Temp_Index int,
						ChildCompanyID int,
						CompanyID int,
						ReceiveValue decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectCharge decimal(18, 4),
						CollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
						)

						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
						(SELECT
							ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
							C.CompanyID,
							C.ParentCompanyID,
							(CASE C.ParentCompanyID
							WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.Rate + CS1.Charge
							ELSE (@WithdrawAmount * 0.01 * (CS1.Rate - CS2.Rate) + CS1.Charge - CS2.Charge)
							END),
							CS1.Rate,
							CS1.Charge,
							CS2.Rate,
							CS2.Charge
						FROM CompanyTable AS C
						LEFT JOIN WithdrawLimit AS CS1
							ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID
							AND CS1.ServiceType = @AgentServiceType
							AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND CS1.WithdrawLimitType = @WithdrawLimitType
							LEFT JOIN WithdrawLimit AS CS2
							ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID
							AND CS2.ServiceType = @AgentServiceType
							AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND CS2.WithdrawLimitType = @WithdrawLimitType
						WHERE C.SortKey <> ''
						AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey + '%'
						)


						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING (SELECT
						T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
						ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue, 0) AS ReceiveValue,
						ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue, 0) AS PayValue
						FROM @TempAgent AS T1
						RIGHT JOIN @TempAgent AS T2
						ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID) AS Accounting
						ON (SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
						)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
						--該下發給該代理的淨額
						UPDATE SET
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
						INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount, forProviderCode)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate,
						@CurrencyType,
						@ServiceType,
						Accounting.CompanyID,
						0,
						0,
						Accounting.ReceiveValue*-1,
						Accounting.PayValue*-1,
						@ProviderCode
						);

						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
						ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate,OrderType)
						SELECT
							@CurrencyType,
							@ServiceType,
							T.CompanyID,
							T.CollectRate,
							T.CollectCharge,
							T.ChildCompanyID,
							T.ChildCollectRate,
							T.ChildCollectCharge,
							T.ReceiveValue*-1,
							@WithdrawID,
							GETDATE(),
							1
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0
					END
			       COMMIT 
				   RETURN 0 
			    END 
				ELSE 
			    BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK 
			        RETURN -4
			    END 
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
