-- ============================================= 
-- Author:       
-- Create date:  
-- Description:   
-- ============================================= 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spReviewWithdrawaltoProccessing] 
  -- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here 
  @WithdrawID INT
AS 
  BEGIN 
      -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from 
      -- interfering with SELECT statements. 
      SET NOCOUNT ON; 

      -- Insert statements for procedure here 
      -- return 
      --  0=success 
      -- -1=交易單不存在 
      -- -2=交易單狀態有誤
      -- -3=鎖定失敗 

	  DECLARE @WithdrawSerial varchar(50)  
	  DECLARE @BeforeStatus INT
	  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255) 
      DECLARE @LockRet INT 

      BEGIN TRAN 

	  --取得提現單資料 
      SELECT @WithdrawSerial = WithdrawSerial,
	         @BeforeStatus=Status
      FROM   Withdrawal WITH (NOLOCK) 
      WHERE  @WithdrawID = WithdrawID

      SET @LockName = 'ReviewWithdrawal' 

      EXEC @LockRet = Sp_getapplock 
             @Resource = @LockName, 
             @LockMode = 'Exclusive', 
             @LockOwner = 'Transaction', 
             @LockTimeout = 10000 

      --RETURN -0 
      IF ( @LockRet >= 0 ) 
      BEGIN 
			  --檢查
	   IF(@WithdrawSerial IS NOT NULL)
	   BEGIN
	  	  IF(@BeforeStatus = 0) 
	 		BEGIN 
			    UPDATE Withdrawal WITH (ROWLOCK) SET Status=1,UpStatus=1 WHERE WithdrawID=@WithdrawID	  
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT>0)
				BEGIN
					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
				END
			END 
		  ELSE 
		  BEGIN
		    ROLLBACK	
			RETURN -2
		  END
	   END		
	   ELSE
	   BEGIN
	   	ROLLBACK	
	  	RETURN -1
	   END
      END 
      ELSE 
	  BEGIN 
		RETURN -3
	  END 
  END 
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetAgentClose]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetAgentClose] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@CompanyID int,
	@CurrencyType varchar(100),
	@ServiceType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=處理筆數為0
	-- -2=鎖定失敗 
	-- -3=加扣點失敗
	DECLARE @_TotalReceiveAmount  decimal(18, 4)	
	DECLARE @_TotolReceiveCount int
	
	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--代理結帳單ID
	DECLARE @AgentCloseID int
	--結算統計日期
	DECLARE @StartDate datetime
	DECLARE @EndDate datetime
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

    SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
    FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
    WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

	--取得佔成單資料
	SELECT @_TotalReceiveAmount= ISNULL(SUM(ReceiveAmount),0),
		   @_TotolReceiveCount= Count(*),
		   @StartDate = MIN(dbo.GetReportDate(PaymentFinishDate,@Timezone)),
		   @EndDate = MAX(dbo.GetReportDate(PaymentFinishDate,@Timezone))
	FROM AgentReceive AR WITH (NOLOCK) 
	WHERE forCompanyID = @CompanyID 
	AND Currency = @CurrencyType 
	AND ReceiveStatus = 0		

	IF(@_TotolReceiveCount >0)
	BEGIN
			BEGIN TRAN

			SET @LockName = 'SetAgentClose'
			EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

			IF (@LockRet >= 0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO AgentClose(Currency, StartDate, EndDate, forCompanyID, TotalReceiveAmount, TotolReceiveCount)
							VALUES (@CurrencyType, @StartDate, @EndDate, @CompanyID, @_TotalReceiveAmount, @_TotolReceiveCount)
				
				SET @AgentCloseID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

				SET @PointDescription = 'AgentClose,AgentCloseID=' + CONVERT(nvarchar(50), @AgentCloseID)

				--加扣點
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 2, @_TotalReceiveAmount, @AgentCloseID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType

				IF (@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE AgentReceive WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET forAgentCloseID= @AgentCloseID,
						ReceiveStatus=1							
					WHERE forCompanyID = @CompanyID 
					AND Currency = @CurrencyType 
					AND dbo.GetReportDate(PaymentFinishDate,@Timezone) >= @StartDate
					AND dbo.GetReportDate(PaymentFinishDate,@Timezone) <= @EndDate
					AND ReceiveStatus = 0	

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK			
					RETURN -3
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK			
				RETURN -2
			END
			COMMIT
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetManualPayment]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetManualPayment] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@PaymentSerial varchar(50),
	@ProcessStatus int,
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@ServiceType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=交易單 不存在
	-- -2=交易資料有誤 
	-- -3=供應商，交易失敗
	-- -4=鎖定失敗
	-- -5=加扣點失敗
	-- -6=未通知廠商，請通知廠商
	-- -7=交易單非可修改之狀態
	-- -8=通知營運商失敗單，請再次通知
	-- -9=订单审核中
	DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
	DECLARE @InsideLevel int
	DECLARE	@PaymentID int
	DECLARE @CompanyID  int	
	DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)

	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--儲值總額度
	DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--交易狀態
	DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
	--收入額度，營運商
	DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--成本額度，供應商
	DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--毛利
	DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--計算相關數值
	DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	--結算統計日期
	DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
	DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @FinishDate=getdate()
	
	--取得交易資料
	SELECT @PaymentID = PaymentID, 
	 @CompanyID=forCompanyID,
	 @CurrencyType=CurrencyType,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @OrderAmount=OrderAmount,
	 @CreateDate=CreateDate,
	 @CostRate=CostRate,
	 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
	 @CollectRate=CollectRate,
	 @CollectCharge=CollectCharge,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @OrderAmount=OrderAmount
	FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE PaymentSerial=@PaymentSerial		

	--取得代理相關資料
	SELECT @InsideLevel=InsideLevel,
	 @SortKey=SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone
	FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID		

    SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	IF(@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		IF(@OldProcessStatus = 1)
		BEGIN
			--交易成功
			IF(@ProcessStatus = 4)
			BEGIN
				BEGIN TRAN

				SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
				EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

				--RETURN -0
				IF (@LockRet >= 0)
				BEGIN				
					SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentManualSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
				--營運商實際增加的可用額度
					SET @PaymentAmount = @OrderAmount - (@OrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
				--供應商實際增加的下發額度
					SET @CostAmount = @OrderAmount - (@OrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
				--毛利
					SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
					--加扣點
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 1, @PaymentAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType
					
					IF (@PointRet = 0)
					BEGIN
					
					--更新交易單狀態
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, BankSequenceID='', PaymentResult='', FinishDate = @FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PaymentAmount=@PaymentAmount, PartialOrderAmount=@OrderAmount,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
					
					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @OrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount					
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @OrderAmount, @CostAmount)
					END

					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, @CostAmount,@ProfitAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription
					IF (@PointRet <> 0)
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -4
					END


					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @OrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
						SummaryProviderNetAmount=SummaryProviderNetAmount+@CostAmount				
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount,forProviderCode,SummaryProviderNetAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @OrderAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0,@ProviderCode,@CostAmount)
					END

					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel>0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE
						(
							Temp_Index			int,
							ChildCompanyID		int,
							CompanyID			int,
							ReceiveValue		decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectRate	decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectCharge	decimal(18, 4),
							CollectRate			decimal(18, 4),
							CollectCharge		decimal(18, 4)
						)
						
						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
							( SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
									 C.CompanyID,
									 C.ParentCompanyID,
									 (CASE C.ParentCompanyID
										WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate +  CS1.CollectCharge
										ELSE (@OrderAmount * 0.01 *(CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
										END ),
									CS1.CollectRate,
									CS1.CollectCharge,
									CS2.CollectRate,
									CS2.CollectCharge
							  FROM CompanyTable AS C
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1 ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			 
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2 ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			
							  WHERE C.SortKey <> '' AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey+'%' 				
							 )
						
						
						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING(
							SELECT	T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
									ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue,0) AS ReceiveValue,
									ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue,0) AS PayValue
							FROM @TempAgent AS T1
							RIGHT JOIN  @TempAgent AS T2 ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID
							) As Accounting
						ON	(SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
							)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
							--該下發給該代理的淨額
							UPDATE SET 
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
							INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount,forProviderCode)
							VALUES (@SummaryDate, 
									@CurrencyType,
									@ServiceType,
									Accounting.CompanyID,
									0,
									0,
									Accounting.ReceiveValue,
									Accounting.PayValue,
									@ProviderCode
									);
						
						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
											 ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)  
						SELECT @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, T.CompanyID, T.CollectRate, T.CollectCharge, T.ChildCompanyID , T.ChildCollectRate, T.ChildCollectCharge,
								T.ReceiveValue, @PaymentID, @FinishDate     
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0

					
					END

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						IF(@PointRet<>-3)	
							UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=6, PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0,forPaymentSerial=@PointRet WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
						RETURN -5
					END
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK						
						UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=3, PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0 WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
					RETURN -4
				END
			END
		
			--BEGIN
			--	--供應商回傳之金額是否一致
			--	IF(@OrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount)
			
			--	ELSE
			--	BEGIN
			--		UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=5, BankSequenceID=@BankSequenceID, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID,PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
			--		RETURN -2
			--	END
			--END
			--交易失敗
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, BankSequenceID='', PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
				RETURN 0
			END
		END				
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			RETURN -9
		END
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetManualProviderPayment]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetManualProviderPayment] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@PaymentSerial varchar(50),
	@ProcessStatus int

AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=交易單 不存在
	-- -2=交易資料有誤 
	-- -3=供應商，交易失敗
	-- -4=鎖定失敗
	-- -5=加扣點失敗
	-- -6=未通知廠商，請通知廠商
	-- -7=交易單非可修改之狀態
	-- -8=通知營運商失敗單，請再次通知
	-- -9=订单审核中
	DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
	DECLARE @InsideLevel int
	DECLARE	@PaymentID int
	DECLARE @CompanyID  int	
	DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
	DECLARE	@ProviderCode varchar(20)
	DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20)
	--专属供应商
	DECLARE @ProxyProviderRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @GroupID int
	DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--儲值總額度
	DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--交易狀態
	DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
	--收入額度，營運商
	DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--成本額度，供應商
	DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--毛利
	DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--計算相關數值
	DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	--結算統計日期
	DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
	DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @FinishDate=getdate()
	
	--取得交易資料
	SELECT @PaymentID = PaymentID, 
	 @CompanyID=forCompanyID,
	 @CurrencyType=CurrencyType,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @OrderAmount=OrderAmount,
	 @CreateDate=CreateDate,
	 @CostRate=CostRate,
	 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
	 @CollectRate=CollectRate,
	 @CollectCharge=CollectCharge,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @ProviderCode =ProviderCode,
	 @ServiceType =ServiceType
	FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE PaymentSerial=@PaymentSerial		

	 SELECT @ProxyProviderRate = PaymentRate,
	        @GroupID=GroupID
	FROM ProxyProviderOrder WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE forOrderSerial=@PaymentSerial		

	--取得代理相關資料
	SELECT @InsideLevel=InsideLevel,
	 @SortKey=SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone
	FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID		

    SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	IF(@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		IF(@OldProcessStatus = 8)
		BEGIN
			--交易成功
			IF(@ProcessStatus = 4)
			BEGIN
				BEGIN TRAN

				SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
				EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 10000

				--RETURN -0
				IF (@LockRet >= 0)
				BEGIN			
				    --取得专属供应商群组额度
					SELECT @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
					FROM   ProxyProviderGroup WITH (NOLOCK) 
					WHERE  GroupID = @GroupID AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
					--取得专属供应商额度
					SELECT @ProxyCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
					FROM   ProxyProvider WITH (NOLOCK) 
					WHERE  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 
					
					SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentManualSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
			    	--營運商實際增加的可用額度
					SET @PaymentAmount = @OrderAmount - (@OrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
			    	--供應商實際增加的下發額度
					SET @CostAmount = @OrderAmount - (@OrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
			    	--毛利
					SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
					--加扣點
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 1, @PaymentAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType
					
					IF (@PointRet = 0)
					BEGIN
					
					--更新交易單狀態
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, BankSequenceID='', PaymentResult='', FinishDate = @FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PaymentAmount=@PaymentAmount, PartialOrderAmount=@OrderAmount  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
					
					-- 更新代理供应商钱包金额与代理供应商可用额度
					UPDATE ProxyProvider WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint + (@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01),
					    CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint + (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01))					
					WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode

					--更新专属供应商群组额度
					UPDATE ProxyProviderGroup WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET ProxyProviderPoint=ProxyProviderPoint + (@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01),
					    CanUsePoint=CanUsePoint + (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01)),
						ProfitAmount=ProfitAmount+@ProfitAmount
					WHERE forProviderCode=@ProviderCode And GroupID=@GroupID

					
					-- 更新属供供應商每日統計
					UPDATE ProxySummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @OrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01)),
							PaymentCount=PaymentCount+1					
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
							CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
							ProviderCode=@ProviderCode AND 
							GroupID=@GroupID 
						 
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO ProxySummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount,GroupID,PaymentCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, @OrderAmount, (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01)),@GroupID,1)
					END

					--新增专属供应商流水资料
							INSERT INTO ProxyProviderPointHistory(CurrencyType,ProviderCode, OperatorType, Value, BeforeValue,GroupValue,GroupBeforeValue, Description, TransactionID,GroupID)
			             	SELECT                             @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01)), @ProxyCanUsePoint, (@OrderAmount-(@OrderAmount *@ProxyProviderRate* 0.01)), @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint,'', @PaymentID,@GroupID
		

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @OrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount					
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @OrderAmount, @CostAmount)
					END

					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, @CostAmount,@ProfitAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription
					IF (@PointRet <> 0)
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -4
					END


					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @OrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
						SummaryProviderNetAmount=SummaryProviderNetAmount+@CostAmount				
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount,forProviderCode,SummaryProviderNetAmount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @OrderAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0,@ProviderCode,@CostAmount)
					END

					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel>0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE
						(
							Temp_Index			int,
							ChildCompanyID		int,
							CompanyID			int,
							ReceiveValue		decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectRate	decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectCharge	decimal(18, 4),
							CollectRate			decimal(18, 4),
							CollectCharge		decimal(18, 4)
						)
						
						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
							( SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
									 C.CompanyID,
									 C.ParentCompanyID,
									 (CASE C.ParentCompanyID
										WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate +  CS1.CollectCharge
										ELSE (@OrderAmount * 0.01 *(CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
										END ),
									CS1.CollectRate,
									CS1.CollectCharge,
									CS2.CollectRate,
									CS2.CollectCharge
							  FROM CompanyTable AS C
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1 ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			 
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2 ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			
							  WHERE C.SortKey <> '' AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey+'%' 				
							 )
						
						
						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING(
							SELECT	T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
									ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue,0) AS ReceiveValue,
									ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue,0) AS PayValue
							FROM @TempAgent AS T1
							RIGHT JOIN  @TempAgent AS T2 ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID
							) As Accounting
						ON	(SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
							)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
							--該下發給該代理的淨額
							UPDATE SET 
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
							INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount,forProviderCode)
							VALUES (@SummaryDate, 
									@CurrencyType,
									@ServiceType,
									Accounting.CompanyID,
									0,
									0,
									Accounting.ReceiveValue,
									Accounting.PayValue,
									@ProviderCode
									);
						
						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
											 ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)  
						SELECT @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, T.CompanyID, T.CollectRate, T.CollectCharge, T.ChildCompanyID , T.ChildCollectRate, T.ChildCollectCharge,
								T.ReceiveValue, @PaymentID, @FinishDate     
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0

					
					END

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						IF(@PointRet<>-3)	
							UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=6, PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0,forPaymentSerial=@PointRet WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
						RETURN -5
					END
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK						
						UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=3, PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0 WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
					RETURN -4
				END
			END
		
			--BEGIN
			--	--供應商回傳之金額是否一致
			--	IF(@OrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount)
			
			--	ELSE
			--	BEGIN
			--		UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=5, BankSequenceID=@BankSequenceID, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID,PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
			--		RETURN -2
			--	END
			--END
			--交易失敗
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProviderCode=@ProviderCode,ServiceType=@ServiceType,ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, BankSequenceID='', PaymentResult='', FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID='', PartialOrderAmount=0  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
				RETURN 0
			END
		END				
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			RETURN -9
		END
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetPaymentProcessStatus_ver4]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetPaymentProcessStatus_ver4] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@PaymentSerial varchar(50),
	@ProcessStatus int,
	@PaymentResult nvarchar(MAX),
	@BankSequenceID varchar(100),
	@ProviderOrderAmount decimal(18, 4),
	@ProviderOrderID varchar(200)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=交易單 不存在
	-- -2=交易資料有誤 
	-- -3=供應商，交易失敗
	-- -4=鎖定失敗
	-- -5=加扣點失敗
	-- -6=未通知廠商，請通知廠商
	-- -7=交易單非可修改之狀態
	-- -8=通知營運商失敗單，請再次通知
	DECLARE	@PaymentID int
	DECLARE @CompanyID  int	
	DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
	DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
	DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20)
	DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
	DECLARE @InsideLevel int

	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--儲值總額度
	DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--交易狀態
	DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
	--收入額度，營運商
	DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--成本額度，供應商
	DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @isPrePaidCostAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--毛利
	DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--計算相關數值
	DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT
	--結算統計日期
	DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
	DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @isPrePaid int

	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @FinishDate=getdate()
	
	--取得交易資料
	SELECT @PaymentID = PaymentID, 
	 @CompanyID=forCompanyID,
	 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
	 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
	 @CurrencyType=CurrencyType,
	 @CostRate=CostRate,
	 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
	 @CollectRate=CollectRate,
	 @CollectCharge=CollectCharge,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @OrderAmount=OrderAmount,
	 @CreateDate=CreateDate
	FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE PaymentSerial=@PaymentSerial
	
	--取得小數處理
	SELECT @DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces,@isPrePaid=isPrePaid
	FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
	WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

	--取得代理相關資料
	SELECT @InsideLevel=InsideLevel,
	 @SortKey=SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone
	FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID		

    SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	IF(@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		IF(@OldProcessStatus = 1 or @OldProcessStatus = 6 or @OldProcessStatus = 8)
		BEGIN
			--交易成功
			IF(@ProcessStatus = 2)
			BEGIN
				BEGIN TRAN

				SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
				EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 10000

				--RETURN -0
				IF (@LockRet >= 0)
				BEGIN				
					SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
			
					--處理小數點部分與廠商一至,無條件捨去
					IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
					BEGIN
					  	--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - floor(@ProviderOrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - floor(@ProviderOrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
					  	--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
					END

					--毛利
					SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
					--加扣點
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 1, @PaymentAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType
					
					IF (@PointRet = 0)
					BEGIN
					
					--更新交易單狀態
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=2, BankSequenceID=@BankSequenceID, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate = @FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PaymentAmount=@PaymentAmount, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,0)  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
					
					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount=SuccessCount + 1							
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @ProviderOrderAmount, @CostAmount,1)
					END

					--- 更新供應商錢包額度
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, @CostAmount, @ProfitAmount, @PaymentID, @PointDescription
					IF (@PointRet <> 0)
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						RETURN -9
					END

					--- 更新供應商預付數據
					IF (@isPrePaid =0)
					BEGIN
				    	SET @isPrePaidCostAmount=@CostAmount*-1
						EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPrePaidPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, @isPrePaidCostAmount, @PaymentID, @PointDescription
						IF (@PointRet <> 0)
						BEGIN
							ROLLBACK	
							RETURN -10
						END
					END


					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
						SummaryProviderNetAmount=SummaryProviderNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount=SuccessCount + 1							
					WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
					      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
						  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
						  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
						  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
					IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount,forProviderCode,SummaryProviderNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @ProviderOrderAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0,@ProviderCode,@CostAmount,1)
					END

					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel>0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE
						(
							Temp_Index			int,
							ChildCompanyID		int,
							CompanyID			int,
							ReceiveValue		decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectRate	decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectCharge	decimal(18, 4),
							CollectRate			decimal(18, 4),
							CollectCharge		decimal(18, 4)
						)
						
						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
							( SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
									 C.CompanyID,
									 C.ParentCompanyID,
									 (CASE C.ParentCompanyID
										WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate +  CS1.CollectCharge
										ELSE (@ProviderOrderAmount * 0.01 *(CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
										END ),
									CS1.CollectRate,
									CS1.CollectCharge,
									CS2.CollectRate,
									CS2.CollectCharge
							  FROM CompanyTable AS C
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1 ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			 
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2 ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			
							  WHERE C.SortKey <> '' AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey+'%' 				
							 )
						
						
						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING(
							SELECT	T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
									ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue,0) AS ReceiveValue,
									ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue,0) AS PayValue
							FROM @TempAgent AS T1
							RIGHT JOIN  @TempAgent AS T2 ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID
							) As Accounting
						ON	(SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
							)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
							--該下發給該代理的淨額
							UPDATE SET 
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
							INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount,forProviderCode)
							VALUES (@SummaryDate, 
									@CurrencyType,
									@ServiceType,
									Accounting.CompanyID,
									0,
									0,
									Accounting.ReceiveValue,
									Accounting.PayValue,
									@ProviderCode
									);
						
						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
											 ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)  
						SELECT @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, T.CompanyID, T.CollectRate, T.CollectCharge, T.ChildCompanyID , T.ChildCollectRate, T.ChildCollectCharge,
								T.ReceiveValue, @PaymentID, @FinishDate     
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0

					
					END

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK	
						IF(@PointRet<>-3)	
							UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=6, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,forPaymentSerial=@PointRet,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone) WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
						RETURN -5
					END
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK						
						UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=6, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone) WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
					RETURN -4
				END
			END
		
			--BEGIN
			--	--供應商回傳之金額是否一致
			--	IF(@OrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount)
			
			--	ELSE
			--	BEGIN
			--		UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=5, BankSequenceID=@BankSequenceID, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID,PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
			--		RETURN -2
			--	END
			--END
			--交易失敗
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				--UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, BankSequenceID=@BankSequenceID, PaymentResult=@PaymentResult, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 
				RETURN -3
			END
		END				
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			IF(@OldProcessStatus = 2)
				IF(@ProcessStatus = 4)
					BEGIN
					UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=@ProcessStatus, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 

					RETURN 0
					END
				ELSE IF(@ProcessStatus = 2)
					RETURN -8
				ELSE
					RETURN -6
			ELSE
				RETURN -7
		END
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetPaymentToFail]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


-- =============================================
-- Author:  	<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetPaymentToFail]
-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
@PaymentSerial varchar(255),
@forAdminID int
AS
BEGIN
  -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
  -- interfering with SELECT statements.
  SET NOCOUNT ON;

  -- Insert statements for procedure here
  -- return
  --  0=success
  -- -1=交易單 不存在
  -- -2=交易資料有誤 

  -- -4=鎖定失敗
  -- -5=加扣點失敗
  -- -6=調整供應商預付額度失敗
  DECLARE @PaymentID int
  DECLARE @CompanyID int
  DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20)
  DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
  DECLARE @InsideLevel int

  --扣點紀錄的描述
  DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
  --儲值總額度
  DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --@實際儲值額度
  DECLARE @ProviderOrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
  
  --交易狀態
  DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
  --收入額度，營運商
  DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --成本額度，供應商
  DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @isPrePaidCostAmount decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @isPrePaid int
  --毛利
  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --加扣點回傳值
  DECLARE @PointRet int
  --計算相關數值
  DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT
  --結算統計日期
  DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
  DECLARE @LockRet int

  --专属供应商
  DECLARE @ProxyProviderPaymentRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @GroupID int
  DECLARE @CollectType int
  DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
  --提現額度(专属供应商)
  DECLARE @ProxyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
  --取得統計日期
  SELECT
    @FinishDate = GETDATE()



  BEGIN TRAN

    SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
    EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource = @LockName,
                                  @LockMode = 'Exclusive',
                                  @LockOwner = 'Transaction',
                                  @LockTimeout = 3000
    IF (@LockRet >= 0)
    BEGIN
        --取得交易單資料
		SELECT
		@CompanyID = forCompanyID,
		@PaymentID = PaymentID,
		@ProviderCode = ProviderCode,
		@ServiceType = ServiceType,
		@CurrencyType = CurrencyType,
		@CostRate = CostRate,
		@CostCharge = CostCharge,
		@CollectRate = CollectRate,
		@CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
		@OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
		@OrderAmount = OrderAmount,
		@CreateDate = CreateDate,
		@ProviderOrderAmount=PartialOrderAmount
		FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE PaymentSerial = @PaymentSerial

		--取得代理相關資料
		SELECT
		@InsideLevel = InsideLevel,
		@SortKey = SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone
		FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID

		SELECT
		@SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

		--取得供应商资料
		SELECT @CollectType = CollectType,@DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces,@isPrePaid=isPrePaid
		FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

		IF @CollectType=1 
		BEGIN
		SELECT @ProxyProviderPaymentRate = PaymentRate,
				@GroupID = GroupID
		FROM   ProxyProviderOrder WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forOrderSerial = @PaymentSerial  And Type=0
		END

		IF (@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			IF (@OldProcessStatus = 2 OR @OldProcessStatus = 4)
			BEGIN
				SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
				--處理小數點部分與廠商一至,無條件捨去
				IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
					--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = (@ProviderOrderAmount - floor(@ProviderOrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge)*-1
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = (@ProviderOrderAmount - floor(@ProviderOrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge) *-1
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
						--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = (@ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge)*-1
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = (@ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge)*-1
				END

				--實際充值額度
				SET @ProviderOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount*-1
				--毛利
				SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
				--更新商戶錢包
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyManual 0,
													@ServiceType,
													@CurrencyType,
													@PaymentAmount,
													N'充值单转失敗',
													@PaymentSerial,
													@CompanyID,
													@forAdminID
                IF (@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderManual 1,
												 	@ProviderCode,
													@CurrencyType,
													@CostAmount,
													N'充值单转失敗',
													@PaymentSerial,
													@forAdminID
					IF (@PointRet <> 0)
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK
						RETURN -5
					END

				
					IF (@isPrePaid =0)
					BEGIN
				    	SET @isPrePaidCostAmount=@CostAmount*-1
						EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPrePaidPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 2, @isPrePaidCostAmount, @PaymentID, @PointDescription
						IF (@PointRet <> 0)
						BEGIN
							ROLLBACK
							RETURN -6
						END
					END
					
					--更新交易單狀態
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
					SET ProcessStatus = 3,
						FinishDate = @FinishDate,
						PaymentAmount = 0,
						PartialOrderAmount = 0,
						FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
					WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK)
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryAmount = SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount = SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount = SuccessCount - 1
					WHERE SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
					AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
					AND ServiceType = @ServiceType
					AND ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

					IF (@@ROWCOUNT <= 0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @ProviderOrderAmount, @CostAmount,0)
					END

					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK)
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryAmount = SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount = SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
						SummaryProviderNetAmount = SummaryProviderNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount = SuccessCount - 1
					WHERE SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
					AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
					AND ServiceType = @ServiceType
					AND forCompanyID = @CompanyID
					AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode

					IF (@@ROWCOUNT <= 0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, forProviderCode, SummaryProviderNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @ProviderOrderAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0, @ProviderCode, @CostAmount,0)
					END

					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel > 0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE (
						Temp_Index int,
						ChildCompanyID int,
						CompanyID int,
						ReceiveValue decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectCharge decimal(18, 4),
						CollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
						)

						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
						(SELECT
							ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
							C.CompanyID,
							C.ParentCompanyID,
							(CASE C.ParentCompanyID
							WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate + CS1.CollectCharge
							ELSE (@ProviderOrderAmount * 0.01 * (CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
							END),
							CS1.CollectRate,
							CS1.CollectCharge,
							CS2.CollectRate,
							CS2.CollectCharge
						FROM CompanyTable AS C
						LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1
							ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID
							AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2
							ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID
							AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						WHERE C.SortKey <> ''
						AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey + '%'
						)


						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING (SELECT
						T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
						ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue, 0) AS ReceiveValue,
						ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue, 0) AS PayValue
						FROM @TempAgent AS T1
						RIGHT JOIN @TempAgent AS T2
						ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID) AS Accounting
						ON (SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
						)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
						--該下發給該代理的淨額
						UPDATE SET
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
						INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount, forProviderCode)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate,
						@CurrencyType,
						@ServiceType,
						Accounting.CompanyID,
						0,
						0,
						Accounting.ReceiveValue*-1,
						Accounting.PayValue*-1,
						@ProviderCode
						);

						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
						ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)
						SELECT
							@CurrencyType,
							@ServiceType,
							T.CompanyID,
							T.CollectRate,
							T.CollectCharge,
							T.ChildCompanyID,
							T.ChildCollectRate,
							T.ChildCollectCharge,
							T.ReceiveValue,
							@PaymentID,
							@FinishDate
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0
					END

					COMMIT
					RETURN 0
				
			    END
				ELSE	
				BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
					SET ProcessStatus = 3,
					FinishDate = @FinishDate,
					PartialOrderAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount,
					FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
					WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
					RETURN -3
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
				SET ProcessStatus = 6,
					FinishDate = @FinishDate,
					PartialOrderAmount = 0,
					FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
				WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
				RETURN -2
			END
		END
		ELSE
			ROLLBACK
			UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
			SET ProcessStatus = 6,
			FinishDate = @FinishDate,
			PartialOrderAmount = 0,
			FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
			WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
			RETURN -1
		END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK
		UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
		SET ProcessStatus = 6,
			FinishDate = @FinishDate,
			PartialOrderAmount = 0,
			FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
		WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
		RETURN -4
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetPaymentToSuccess]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

-- =============================================
-- Author:  	<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetPaymentToSuccess]
-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
@PaymentSerial varchar(255),
@RealAmount decimal(18, 4),
@forAdminID int
AS
BEGIN
  -- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
  -- interfering with SELECT statements.
  SET NOCOUNT ON;

  -- Insert statements for procedure here
  -- return
  --  0=success
  -- -1=交易單 不存在
  -- -2=交易資料有誤 

  -- -4=鎖定失敗
  -- -5=加扣點失敗
  -- -6=調整供應商預付額度失敗
  DECLARE @PaymentID int
  DECLARE @CompanyID int
  DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
  DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
  DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20)
  DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
  DECLARE @InsideLevel int

  --扣點紀錄的描述
  DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
  --儲值總額度
  DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --交易狀態
  DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
  --收入額度，營運商
  DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --成本額度，供應商
  DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @isPrePaidCostAmount decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @isPrePaid int
  --毛利
  DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
  --加扣點回傳值
  DECLARE @PointRet int
  --計算相關數值
  DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @DecimalPlaces INT
  --結算統計日期
  DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
  DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
  DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
  DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
  DECLARE @LockRet int

  --专属供应商
  DECLARE @ProxyProviderPaymentRate decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @GroupID int
  DECLARE @CollectType int
  DECLARE @ProxyGroupCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
  DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
  --提現額度(专属供应商)
  DECLARE @ProxyFinishDidAmount decimal(18, 4) 
  DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
  --銀行卡
  DECLARE @LockGUID varchar(50)
  --取得統計日期
  SELECT
    @FinishDate = GETDATE()

  BEGIN TRAN

    SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
    EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource = @LockName,
                                  @LockMode = 'Exclusive',
                                  @LockOwner = 'Transaction',
                                  @LockTimeout = 3000
    IF (@LockRet >= 0)
    BEGIN
        --取得交易單資料
		SELECT
		@CompanyID = forCompanyID,
		@PaymentID = PaymentID,
		@ProviderCode = ProviderCode,
		@ServiceType = ServiceType,
		@CurrencyType = CurrencyType,
		@CostRate = CostRate,
		@CostCharge = CostCharge,
		@CollectRate = CollectRate,
		@CollectCharge = CollectCharge,
		@OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
		@OrderAmount = OrderAmount,
		@CreateDate = CreateDate
		FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE PaymentSerial = @PaymentSerial

		--取得代理相關資料
		SELECT
		@InsideLevel = InsideLevel,
		@SortKey = SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone

		FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID
		
		SELECT
		@SummaryDate = dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

		--取得供应商资料
		SELECT @CollectType = CollectType,@DecimalPlaces=DecimalPlaces,@isPrePaid=isPrePaid
		FROM   ProviderCode WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

		IF @CollectType=1 
		BEGIN
		SELECT @ProxyProviderPaymentRate = PaymentRate,
				@GroupID = GroupID
		FROM   ProxyProviderOrder WITH (NOLOCK) 
		WHERE  forOrderSerial = @PaymentSerial  And Type=0
		END

		--取得鎖定銀行卡 GUID
		SELECT @LockGUID=forBankCardLockID
		FROM PaymentDetailBankCard WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE forPaymentID=@PaymentID

		IF (@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			IF (@OldProcessStatus = 1)
			BEGIN		   
				SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
			
				--處理小數點部分與廠商一至,無條件捨去
				IF(@DecimalPlaces=1)
				BEGIN
					--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = (@RealAmount - floor(@RealAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge)
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = (@RealAmount - floor(@RealAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge)
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
						--營運商實際增加的可用額度
						SET @PaymentAmount = @RealAmount - (@RealAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
						--供應商實際增加的下發額度
						SET @CostAmount = @RealAmount - (@RealAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
				END


				--毛利
				SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
				--更新商戶錢包
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyManual 0,
													@ServiceType,
													@CurrencyType,
													@PaymentAmount,
													N'充值单转成功',
													@PaymentSerial,
													@CompanyID,
													@forAdminID
                IF (@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
					---- 更新供應商錢包數據
					EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderManualByUpdatePaymentStatus 1,
												 		 @ProviderCode,
														 @CurrencyType,
														 @CostAmount,
														 N'失败转成功单',
														 @PaymentSerial,
														 @forAdminID,
														 @ProfitAmount
					IF (@PointRet <> 0)
					BEGIN
						ROLLBACK
						RETURN -5
					END

				   IF (@isPrePaid =0)
					BEGIN
				    	SET @isPrePaidCostAmount=@CostAmount*-1
						EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPrePaidPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 2, @isPrePaidCostAmount, @PaymentID, @PointDescription
						IF (@PointRet <> 0)
						BEGIN
							ROLLBACK
							RETURN -6
						END
					END

					--更新交易單狀態
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
					SET ProcessStatus = 2,
						FinishDate = @FinishDate,
						PaymentAmount = @PaymentAmount,
						PartialOrderAmount = @RealAmount,
						FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
					WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID

					-- 更新供應商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK)
					--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
					SET SummaryAmount = SummaryAmount + @RealAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount = SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount = SuccessCount + 1
					WHERE SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
					AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
					AND ServiceType = @ServiceType
					AND ProviderCode = @ProviderCode

					IF (@@ROWCOUNT <= 0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @RealAmount, @CostAmount,1)
					END

					-- 更新營運商每日統計
					UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK)
					--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
					--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
					SET SummaryAmount = SummaryAmount + @RealAmount,
						SummaryNetAmount = SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
						SummaryProviderNetAmount = SummaryProviderNetAmount + @CostAmount,
						SuccessCount = SuccessCount + 1
					WHERE SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
					AND CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
					AND ServiceType = @ServiceType
					AND forCompanyID = @CompanyID
					AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode

					IF (@@ROWCOUNT <= 0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, forProviderCode, SummaryProviderNetAmount,SuccessCount)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @RealAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0, @ProviderCode, @CostAmount,1)
					END
					--判斷是否有上層代理
					IF (@InsideLevel > 0)
					BEGIN
						--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
						--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
						--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

						--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE (
						Temp_Index int,
						ChildCompanyID int,
						CompanyID int,
						ReceiveValue decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectCharge decimal(18, 4),
						CollectRate decimal(18, 4),
						CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
						)

						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
						(SELECT
							ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
							C.CompanyID,
							C.ParentCompanyID,
							(CASE C.ParentCompanyID
							WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate + CS1.CollectCharge
							ELSE (@RealAmount * 0.01 * (CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
							END),
							CS1.CollectRate,
							CS1.CollectCharge,
							CS2.CollectRate,
							CS2.CollectCharge
						FROM CompanyTable AS C
						LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1
							ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID
							AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
							LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2
							ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID
							AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						WHERE C.SortKey <> ''
						AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey + '%'
						)


						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING (SELECT
						T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
						ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue, 0) AS ReceiveValue,
						ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue, 0) AS PayValue
						FROM @TempAgent AS T1
						RIGHT JOIN @TempAgent AS T2
						ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID) AS Accounting
						ON (SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
						)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
						--該下發給該代理的淨額
						UPDATE SET
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
						INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount, forProviderCode)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate,
						@CurrencyType,
						@ServiceType,
						Accounting.CompanyID,
						0,
						0,
						Accounting.ReceiveValue,
						Accounting.PayValue,
						@ProviderCode
						);

						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
						ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)
						SELECT
							@CurrencyType,
							@ServiceType,
							T.CompanyID,
							T.CollectRate,
							T.CollectCharge,
							T.ChildCompanyID,
							T.ChildCollectRate,
							T.ChildCollectCharge,
							T.ReceiveValue,
							@PaymentID,
							@FinishDate
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0


					END

					COMMIT

					
					IF(@LockGUID IS NOT NULL)
						EXEC spReleaseBankCard_Internal @LockGUID

					RETURN 0
				
			    END
				ELSE	
				BEGIN 
					ROLLBACK
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
					SET ProcessStatus = 3,
					FinishDate = @FinishDate,
					PartialOrderAmount = @RealAmount,
					FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
					WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
					RETURN -3
				END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
				UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
				SET ProcessStatus = 6,
					FinishDate = @FinishDate,
					PartialOrderAmount = 0,
					FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
				WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
				RETURN -2
			END
		END
		ELSE
			ROLLBACK
			UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
			SET ProcessStatus = 6,
			FinishDate = @FinishDate,
			PartialOrderAmount = 0,
			FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
			WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
			RETURN -1
		END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK
		UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK)
		SET ProcessStatus = 6,
			FinishDate = @FinishDate,
			PartialOrderAmount = 0,
			FinishDateUTC=GETUTCDATE()
		WHERE PaymentID = @PaymentID
		RETURN -4
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spSetSupPayment]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/

GO
