-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spSetSupPayment] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@PaymentID int,
	@ProviderOrderAmount decimal(18, 4),
	@ProviderOrderID varchar(200)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return
	--  0=success
	-- -1=交易單 不存在
	-- -2=交易資料有誤 

	-- -4=鎖定失敗
	-- -5=加扣點失敗

	DECLARE @PaymentSerial varchar(50)
	DECLARE @CompanyID  int	
	DECLARE @CurrencyType varchar(20)
	DECLARE @ProviderCode varchar(20)
	DECLARE @ServiceType varchar(20)
	DECLARE @SortKey varchar(800)
	DECLARE @InsideLevel int

	--扣點紀錄的描述
	DECLARE @PointDescription varchar(200)
	--儲值總額度
	DECLARE @OrderAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--交易狀態
	DECLARE @OldProcessStatus int
	--收入額度，營運商
	DECLARE @PaymentAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--成本額度，供應商
	DECLARE @CostAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--毛利
	DECLARE @ProfitAmount decimal(18, 4)
	--加扣點回傳值
	DECLARE @PointRet int
	--計算相關數值
	DECLARE @CollectRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CollectCharge decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostRate decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @CostCharge decimal(18, 4)
	--結算統計日期
	DECLARE @FinishDate datetime
	DECLARE @CreateDate datetime
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)

	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @FinishDate=getdate()

	--取得交易單資料
	SELECT 
	 @CompanyID=forCompanyID,
	 @ProviderCode=ProviderCode,
	 @ServiceType=ServiceType,
	 @CurrencyType=CurrencyType,
	 @CostRate=CostRate,
	 @CostCharge=CostCharge,
	 @CollectRate=CollectRate,
	 @CollectCharge=CollectCharge,
	 @OldProcessStatus = ProcessStatus,
	 @OrderAmount=OrderAmount,
	 @CreateDate=CreateDate
	FROM PaymentTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
	--取得代理相關資料
	SELECT @InsideLevel=InsideLevel,
	 @SortKey=SortKey,@Timezone=Timezone
	FROM CompanyTable WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID		

	SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(@CreateDate,@Timezone)

	IF(@OrderAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		IF(@OldProcessStatus = 7 or @OldProcessStatus = 1)
		BEGIN
			BEGIN TRAN

			SET @LockName = 'SetPaymentProcessStatus'
			EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

			--RETURN -0
			IF (@LockRet >= 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PointDescription = 'PaymentSuccess,PaymentSerial=' + @PaymentSerial
				--營運商實際增加的可用額度
				SET @PaymentAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CollectRate * 0.01) - @CollectCharge
				--供應商實際增加的下發額度
				SET @CostAmount = @ProviderOrderAmount - (@ProviderOrderAmount * @CostRate * 0.01) - @CostCharge
				--毛利
				SET @ProfitAmount = @CostAmount - @PaymentAmount
				--加扣點
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddCompanyPoint @CompanyID, @CurrencyType, 1, @PaymentAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription, @ServiceType
				
				IF (@PointRet = 0)
				BEGIN
				
				---- 更新供應商錢包數據
				EXEC @PointRet = spAddProviderPoint @CurrencyType, @ProviderCode, 0, @CostAmount, @ProfitAmount,@PaymentID, @PointDescription
				IF (@PointRet <> 0)
				BEGIN
					ROLLBACK	
					RETURN -6
				END
				--更新交易單狀態
				IF (@OldProcessStatus = 7 )
				BEGIN
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus= 4, FinishDate = @FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PaymentAmount=@PaymentAmount, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,	FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)   WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					UPDATE PaymentTable WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus= 2, FinishDate = @FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PaymentAmount=@PaymentAmount, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,	FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)   WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID
				END

				-- 更新供應商每日統計
				UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
				--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
				--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
				SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
					SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @CostAmount,
					SuccessCount=SuccessCount + 1							
				WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
				      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
					  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
					  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount)
					VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, @ProviderOrderAmount, @CostAmount)
				END

				-- 更新營運商每日統計
				UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
				--SummaryAmount 每日玩家儲值總額
				--SummaryNetAmount 每日可用額度變量
				SET SummaryAmount=SummaryAmount + @ProviderOrderAmount,
					SummaryNetAmount=SummaryNetAmount + @PaymentAmount,
					SummaryProviderNetAmount=SummaryProviderNetAmount + @CostAmount,
					SuccessCount=SuccessCount + 1							
				WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
				      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
					  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
					  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
					  forProviderCode=@ProviderCode
	
				IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount,forProviderCode,SummaryProviderNetAmount)
				    VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID, @ProviderOrderAmount, @PaymentAmount, 0,@ProviderCode,@CostAmount)
				END

				--判斷是否有上層代理
				IF (@InsideLevel>0)
				BEGIN
					--此處處理所有代理的應得款項
					--以訂單方式做紀錄(AgentReceive)，並在結算時再進入錢包與結算動作的歷程(AgentClose)
					--另外該次交易產生的"獲取下線的佔成"、"給予上線的佔成"更新到每日的試算總值當中

					--宣告變數
					DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE
					(
						Temp_Index			int,
						ChildCompanyID		int,
						CompanyID			int,
						ReceiveValue		decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectRate	decimal(18, 4),
						ChildCollectCharge	decimal(18, 4),
						CollectRate			decimal(18, 4),
						CollectCharge		decimal(18, 4)
					)
					
					--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
					INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
						( SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
								 C.CompanyID,
								 C.ParentCompanyID,
								 (CASE C.ParentCompanyID
									WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate +  CS1.CollectCharge
									ELSE (@ProviderOrderAmount * 0.01 *(CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
									END ),
								CS1.CollectRate,
								CS1.CollectCharge,
								CS2.CollectRate,
								CS2.CollectCharge
						  FROM CompanyTable AS C
						  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1 ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS1.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			 
						  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2 ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS2.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType			
						  WHERE C.SortKey <> '' AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey+'%' 				
						 )
					
					
					--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
					MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
					USING(
						SELECT	T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
								ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue,0) AS ReceiveValue,
								ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue,0) AS PayValue
						FROM @TempAgent AS T1
						RIGHT JOIN  @TempAgent AS T2 ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID
						) As Accounting
					ON	(SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = @CurrencyType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
						AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
						)
					WHEN MATCHED THEN
						--該下發給該代理的淨額
						UPDATE SET 
							SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
							SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
					WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
						INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount,forProviderCode)
						VALUES (@SummaryDate, 
								@CurrencyType,
								@ServiceType,
								Accounting.CompanyID,
								0,
								0,
								Accounting.ReceiveValue,
								Accounting.PayValue,
								@ProviderCode
								);
					
					--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
					INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
										 ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)  
					SELECT @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, T.CompanyID, T.CollectRate, T.CollectCharge, T.ChildCompanyID , T.ChildCollectRate, T.ChildCollectCharge,
							T.ReceiveValue, @PaymentID, @FinishDate     
					FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0

				
				END

				COMMIT
				RETURN 0
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK		
					UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=3, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,	FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
				RETURN -5
			END
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				ROLLBACK
					UPDATE PaymentTable  WITH (ROWLOCK) SET ProcessStatus=3, FinishDate=@FinishDate, ProviderOrderID=@ProviderOrderID, PartialOrderAmount=@ProviderOrderAmount,	FinishDateUTC=dbo.GetReportDateAndTime(@FinishDate,@Timezone)  WHERE PaymentID=@PaymentID 				
				RETURN -4
			END
		END			
		ELSE
			RETURN -2
	END
	ELSE
		RETURN -1
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spUpdateSummaryCount]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spUpdateSummaryCount]
	@CompanyID int,
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@ServiceType varchar(20),
	@CurrencyType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	
	SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
    FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
    WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(getdate(),@Timezone)

	BEGIN
		BEGIN TRAN

		SET @LockName = 'UpdateSummaryCount'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
		-- 更新供應商每日統計
			UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
			--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
			SET TotalCount = TotalCount + 1							
			WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
				  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
				  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
			
			IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SuccessCount, TotalCount)
				VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode, 0, 0, 0, 1)
			END

			UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
			--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
			SET TotalCount = TotalCount + 1							
			WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
				  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
				  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
				  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
			
			IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, forProviderCode, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SuccessCount, TotalCount)
				VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode, 0, 0, 0, 1)
			END

			BEGIN
				COMMIT
				RETURN 0
			END		
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[spUpdateSummaryWithdrawalCount]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[spUpdateSummaryWithdrawalCount]
	@CompanyID int,
	@ProviderCode varchar(20),
	@ServiceType varchar(20),
	@CurrencyType varchar(20)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	-- return:
	-- 0=success
	-- -1=營運商點數類型不存在
	-- -2=鎖定失敗
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	DECLARE @LockName nvarchar(255)
	DECLARE @LockRet int
	DECLARE @Timezone decimal(18, 4)
	
	SELECT @Timezone=Timezone
    FROM CompanyTable  WITH (NOLOCK)
    WHERE CompanyID=@CompanyID

	--取得統計日期
	SELECT @SummaryDate=dbo.GetReportDate(getdate(),@Timezone)

	BEGIN
		BEGIN TRAN

		SET @LockName = 'UpdateWithdrawalSummaryCount'
		EXEC @LockRet = sp_getapplock @Resource=@LockName, @LockMode='Exclusive', @LockOwner='Transaction', @LockTimeout = 3000

		IF (@LockRet>=0)
		BEGIN
		-- 更新供應商每日統計
			UPDATE SummaryProviderByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
			--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
			SET TotalCount = TotalCount + 1							
			WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
				  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
				  ProviderCode=@ProviderCode
			
			IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO SummaryProviderByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, ProviderCode, WithdrawalTotalCount)
				VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @ProviderCode,1)
			END

			UPDATE SummaryCompanyByDate WITH (ROWLOCK) 
			--SummaryAmount 儲值總額
			--SummaryNetAmount 儲值下發的淨額度
			SET TotalCount = TotalCount + 1							
			WHERE SummaryDate=@SummaryDate AND
			      CurrencyType=@CurrencyType AND
				  ServiceType=@ServiceType AND
				  forCompanyID=@CompanyID AND
				  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
			
			IF (@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO SummaryCompanyByDate (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, forProviderCode, WithdrawalTotalCount)
				VALUES (@SummaryDate, @CurrencyType, @ServiceType, @CompanyID,@ProviderCode,1)
			END

			BEGIN
				COMMIT
				RETURN 0
			END		
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			ROLLBACK
			RETURN -2
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** 物件:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[TestAgentAmount]    指令碼日期: 2026/6/29 上午 10:45:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TestAgentAmount] 
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@ProviderOrderAmount decimal(18, 4),
	@ServiceType varchar(100),
	@SortKey varchar(100),
	@ProviderCode varchar(100)

		 
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @GroupID INT
	 DECLARE @ProxyCanUsePoint decimal(18, 4)
	DECLARE @SummaryDate datetime
	SELECT @SummaryDate=getdate()

		--取得专属供应商群组额度
					SELECT @ProxyCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
					FROM   ProxyProviderGroup WITH (NOLOCK) 
					WHERE  GroupID = @GroupID AND forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 

					RETURN @ProxyCanUsePoint
				--取得专属供应商额度
				SELECT @ProxyCanUsePoint = CanUsePoint 
				FROM   ProxyProvider WITH (NOLOCK) 
				WHERE  forProviderCode = @ProviderCode 


				RETURN @ProxyCanUsePoint
    -- Insert statements for procedure here
	--宣告變數
						DECLARE @TempAgent TABLE
						(
							Temp_Index			int,
							ChildCompanyID		int,
							CompanyID			int,
							ReceiveValue		decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectRate	decimal(18, 4),
							ChildCollectCharge	decimal(18, 4),
							CollectRate			decimal(18, 4),
							CollectCharge		decimal(18, 4)
						)
						
						--把需要處理的資料集選出來丟到Table變數中
						INSERT INTO @TempAgent (Temp_Index, ChildCompanyID, CompanyID, ReceiveValue, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge, CollectRate, CollectCharge)
							( SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY C.CompanyID),
									 C.CompanyID,
									 C.ParentCompanyID,
									 (CASE C.ParentCompanyID
										WHEN 0 THEN 100 * 0.01 * CS1.CollectRate +  CS1.CollectCharge
										ELSE (@ProviderOrderAmount * 0.01 *(CS1.CollectRate - CS2.CollectRate) + CS1.CollectCharge - CS2.CollectCharge)
										END ),
									CS1.CollectRate,
									CS1.CollectCharge,
									CS2.CollectRate,
									CS2.CollectCharge
							  FROM CompanyTable AS C
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS1 ON CS1.forCompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CS1.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS1.CurrencyType = 'CNY'			 
							  LEFT JOIN CompanyService AS CS2 ON CS2.forCompanyID = C.ParentCompanyID AND CS2.ServiceType = @ServiceType AND CS2.CurrencyType = 'CNY'					
							  WHERE C.SortKey <> '' AND @SortKey LIKE C.SortKey+'%' 				
							 )
						
						
						--需要"自己從下線收取的佔成"、"上線從自己收取的佔成"Join成一張表後再做merge動作
						MERGE INTO SummaryCompanyByDate
						USING(
							SELECT	T2.ChildCompanyID AS CompanyID,
									ISNULL(T1.ReceiveValue,0) AS ReceiveValue,
									ISNULL(T2.ReceiveValue,0) AS PayValue
							FROM @TempAgent AS T1
							RIGHT JOIN  @TempAgent AS T2 ON T1.CompanyID = T2.ChildCompanyID
							) As Accounting
						ON	(SummaryCompanyByDate.forCompanyID = Accounting.CompanyID
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.CurrencyType = 'CNY'
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.ServiceType = @ServiceType
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryDate = @SummaryDate
							AND SummaryCompanyByDate.forProviderCode = @ProviderCode
							)
						WHEN MATCHED THEN
							--該下發給該代理的淨額
							UPDATE SET 
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryAgentAmount + Accounting.ReceiveValue,
								SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount = SummaryCompanyByDate.SummaryPayAgentAmount + Accounting.PayValue
						WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN
							INSERT (SummaryDate, CurrencyType, ServiceType, forCompanyID, SummaryAmount, SummaryNetAmount, SummaryAgentAmount, SummaryPayAgentAmount,forProviderCode)
							VALUES (@SummaryDate, 
									'CNY',
									@ServiceType,
									Accounting.CompanyID,
									0,
									0,
									Accounting.ReceiveValue,
									Accounting.PayValue,
									@ProviderCode
									);

						--更新到代理佔成的處理訂單中
						INSERT AgentReceive (Currency, ServiceType, forCompanyID, CollectRate, CollectCharge, forChildCompanyID, ChildCollectRate, ChildCollectCharge,
											 ReceiveAmount, forPaymentID, PaymentFinishDate)  
						SELECT 'CNY', @ServiceType, T.CompanyID, T.CollectRate, T.CollectCharge, T.ChildCompanyID , T.ChildCollectRate, T.ChildCollectCharge,
								T.ReceiveValue, 1, @SummaryDate     
						FROM @TempAgent AS T WHERE T.CompanyID<>0
END
GO
